1b1(phal chen): @##//rgya gar skad du/bud d+ha a ba tad sa ka nA ma ma hA bai pu l+yaM sU tra/bod skad du/sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo/'di skad bdag gis thos pa'i dus gcig na/bcom ldan 'das thog ma mngon par sangs rgyas pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad shin tu rnam par sangs rgyas pa'i thugs mnga' ba/dus gsum mnyam pa nyid du 'phags ma khro gnyer can gyi skabs/
2a1(phal chen): @##//thugs su chud pa'i ye shes kyi sku mnga' ba/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du skus shin tu khyab 
2a2(phal chen): pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du dbyangs kyi yan lag dang gsung gi nga ro shin tu bsgrags pa/mkha' dang nam mkha' lta bu'i 
2a3(phal chen): mtshan nyid mnyam pa nyid du rab tu snang ba'i spyod yul can nam mkha' ltar 'gro ba thams cad kun khyab pa'i snying po byang chub thams 
2a4(phal chen): cad la bzhugs pa'i sku/byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho thams cad kyi nang na rnam par snang ba/'jig rten gyi khams mtha' yas 
2a5(phal chen): cad du nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ltar shar ba/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thamasacad du yongs su skye ba bstan pa/rab tu spros pa dus gsum gyi 
2a6(phal chen): bsod nams rgya chen po rgya mtsho lta bus spyod yul yongs su dag pa/'od kyi dkyil 'khor dang /'od zer kha dog mtha' yas pa mnga' 
2b1(phal chen): ba|chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa'i chos kyi sprin thams cad kyis rnam par 'phrul ba/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du bsgribs pa med pa/sgra'i rtse mo thams cad du mtha' 
2b2(phal chen): dang dbus med par rnam par 'phrul pa byin gyis rlob pa'i mthu mnga' ba/'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba la mngon du gyur par yongs su ston pa/'khor gyi dkyil 
2b3(phal chen): 'khor thams cad du 'khor thams cad du 'phos pa med par gshegs pa/yongs su ston pas khyab par mdzad pa dngos po med pa'i dbyings kyi mtshan nyid thams cad thugs su chud pa/'od 
2b4(phal chen): zer gyi sgo thams cad du dus gsum rnam par sprul pa'i byin gyis rlobs yongs su ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du bskal pa rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab par byin gyis rlabs shin 
2b5(phal chen): tu bsgrubs pa/yul ma ga ta'i chos kyi dgon pa snying po byang chub rdo rje rin po che thams cad ltar gzhom du mi chom par sra ba'i phyogs nor bu rin po che thams cad kyi me tog gis sa gzhi brgyan 
2b6(phal chen): pa nor bu'i rgyal po dri ma med par 'bar bas rab tu bsgrubs pa/rin po che mang po thams cad kyis rnam pa sna tshogs su brgyan pa/yon tan brgya mtsho mtha' yas pa'i rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs pa 
2b7(phal chen): yang dag par bstan pa/nor bu'i rgyal po 'od zer dang /sgra dbyangs kyi rgyal mtshan thams cad kyi rab tu bsgrub pa/spos dang /me tog dang /rin po che'i phreng ba dang /dra ba mang po thams cad rnam par bres pa/
2b8(phal chen): rin po che'i kha dog zad mi shes pa'i char rab tu 'bab pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa yang dag par ston pa'i nor bu'i rgyal pos shin tu bsgrubs pa/me tog sna tshogs chu bo'i klung lta bus yongs su 
3a1(phal chen): @#//bstan pas brgyan pa/rin po che'i shing 'od zer gyi kha dog stug po dang ldan pa'i lag pa brgyan pa/rgyan thams cad du snang ba shin tu 'bar ba'i byin gyi rlabs kyis rnam 
3a2(phal chen): par sprul pa/yang dag par bstan pa'i sa'i snying po/rin po che'i 'bru thams cad 'byung bas phyogs kun tu sbyangs shing brgyan pa'i snying po byang chub dang byang chub chen po'i shing nor bu rin 
3a3(phal chen): po che rdo rje'i rgyan sna tshogs kyi 'bras bus bsgrubs pa'i sdong po dang ldan pa bai Dur+ya dang /nor bu thams cad kyi rigs 'byung ba'i snying po dang ldan pa'i lhun can/yongs su yon tan 
3a4(phal chen): mang po mtha' yas pa'i nor bu rin po che mang po'i 'brus shin tu brgyan cing rab tu 'phel ba'i yal ga can rin po che thams cad kyi lo ma shin tu 'od gsal bas phyogs kyi sprin 
3a5(phal chen): du gyur pa/yal ga thams cad kyi rtse mo las me tog dang /'bras bu'i dra bas yon tan rnam pa sna tshogs can rab tu sbyangs pa/rin po che'i 'bras bu'i sna tshogs shin tu 'bar ba'i 
3a6(phal chen): snying pos/shing gi rgyan me tog thams cad shin tu rgyas shing rnam par gsal ba/phyogs bcur 'od kyi dkyil 'khor rin po che rgya che zhing mang ba'i 'od zer lta bur rnam par snang ba/
3a7(phal chen): nor bu'i yon tan zad mi shes pa'i char chen po rnam par sprul pas yongs su bstan pa/nor bu'i sgo thams cad nas byang chub sems dpa'i sprin 'byung ba shin tu bstan pa/sangs 
3a8(phal chen): rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa dang /byin gyis rlabs kyis zad mi shes pa'i dbyangs 'byung ba'i drung na/de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzhal med khang phyogs thams cad du mngon par snang ba/
3b1(phal chen): rab tu 'phel ba/rin po che'i rgyal po sna tshogs yon tan mtha' yas pa'i ka ba bkye ba/lhun rin po che'i me tog rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa/rgyan mtha' yas pas shin tu 
3b2(phal chen): klub pa'i 'od zer snang bas sprin ltar khebs pa/pho brang gi mtshams thams cad nas 'od kyi rgyal mtshan rab tu bkye ba/byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho mtha' 
3b3(phal chen): yas pa/rjes su 'dod pa'i snying po can bsam gyis mi khyab pa/gsung rgya chen po'i dbyangs dang /sangs rgyas kyi 'od nor bu'i rgyal po lta bu'i dra bas thams cad g.yog pa/sangs 
3b4(phal chen): rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis rnam par sprul pa'i 'phrul ba/dmigs pa thams cad du byung ba/sems can thams cad kyi khyim gyi gzugs brnyan rab tu ston pa/sems skad cig gis 
3b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs thams cad chos kyi dbyings 'byam klas par 'phro ba'i snying po can seng+ge'i khri chen po sangs rgyas kyi gdan du gyur pa/rin po che thams cad dang /yid du 'ong 
3b6(phal chen): ba'i spos sna tshogs kyi snying po can padamo'i dra ba 'od zer mtha' yas pa 'byung bas brten pa/rin po che dri ma med pa'i 'khor lo'i 'od kun tu brdal ba/me tog gi phreng ba bzang zhing yid 
3b7(phal chen): du 'ong ba kha dog mtha' yas pas kun nas klubs pa/nor bu'i 'bru sna tshogs kyi shing gi yal ga las 'od shin tu bkye ba/rta babs dang /khri khri 'phang dang /skar khung khang pa brtsegs 
3b8(phal chen): pa/rnam pa sna tshogs mtha' yas legs par brgyan pa nor bu'i rgyal po 'od zer 'bar ba'i snying po'i sprin thams cad du 'phros pa'i rgyan can/byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho rgya chen 
4a1(phal chen): @#//po'i gtsug gi rin po ches bzung zhing 'od zer rnam par snang ba/phyogs bcu'i mtshams thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi mtshams thams cad du sang rgyas kyi sprul pa'i 
4a2(phal chen): 'od kyi snying po'i nor bu'i rgyal pos shin tu brgyan pa/sangs rgyas kyi yul gyi sgra dbyangs rgya chen pos chos kyi dbyings khyab pa'i byin gyi rlabs dang ldan pa la bzhugs shing /byang chub 
4a3(phal chen): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sngon bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thabs gcig tu spyod spyod pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba 
4a4(phal chen): can/sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che po kun tu bzang po dang /byang chub sems dba' sems 
4a5(phal chen): dpa' chen po dge ba dri ma med pa'i gzi brjid kyi 'od yid du 'ong ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i sems dpa' chen po yon tan 'od mchog kun tu sgron ma dang /byang chub sems 
4a6(phal chen): dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i kun tu 'bar ba seng ge'i rgyal mtshan dang /byang chub sems dad pa'i sems dpa' chen po'i dpal gyi 'od zer kun tu 'od 'phro dang /byang chub sems dang sems 
4a7(phal chen): dpa' chen po yon tan rgya mtsho kun tu dbyangs rgyal mtshan dang /byang cub sems dpa' sems dpa'i chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul ye shes kun tu snang ba'i 'od dang /byang 
4a8(phal chen): chub sems dpa' sems dam pa'i chen po gtsug na rin chen kun tu rgyal mtshan dang /byang chu ba sems dpa' sems dpal chen po rgyal ba'i gsung kun tu 'jam dbyangs dang /byang chub 
4b1(phal chen): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po bsod nams mi zad dri med kun tu gzi brjid dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' che po mtshan gyi 'od zer kun tu snang ba dang /byang chub sems 
4b2(phal chen): dpa' sems dpa' chen po'i 'od zer rgya mtsho 'od chen dpal gyi zla ba dang /byang chub semasadapa' sems dpa' chen po/sprin gyi nga ro rgya mtsho dri med 'od kyi snying po dang /byang chub 
4b3(phal chen): sems dpa' sems dpa' chem po yon tan rin chen 'byung ba ye shes dpal gyi gtsug phud dang /byang chub sems kyi dpa' sems dpa' chen mo rgya chen 'od 'bar blo gros dbang phyug 
4b4(phal chen): dang /byang chub sems dba' sems dpa' chen po pad+mo rnam par gnon pa 'od zer gtsug phud dang /byang  chub sems dpa' sems de ba kun tu ye shes sprin gyi nyi ma'i rgyal 
4b5(phal chen): mtshan snang ba dang /byang chub/sems dpa' sems dpa' sems dpa' ba chen po mthu stobs brtson 'grus rdo rje yangs pa'i snying po dang /byang chub/sems/dpa' sems dpa' chen po spos kyi 'od 'phro shin 
4b6(phal chen): tu 'bar ba'i 'od kyi rgyal mtshan dang /byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa'/chen po rnam par snang mdzad yon tan yangs pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /byang  chub sems/dpa' sems dpa'
4b7(phal chen): chen po bsod nams yangs pa shin tu 'phags pa'i 'od mchog yon tan 'byung ba'i blo gros dang /de dag la sogs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi/byang chub 
4b8(phal chen): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po/kun kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba rgya mtsho gcig gis byung ba pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad kyi 'khor yongs 
5a1(phal chen): @#//su rgyas pas snang bar gyur pa/ye shes kyi mig yongs su dag pa/dus gsum gyi dbyings thams cad du snang bas gsal bar gyur pa/ting nge 'dzin gyi 'khor 
5a2(phal chen): thams cad yongs su dag pas snang bar gyur pa/tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa/rgya mtsho yangs shing zad mi shes pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang mthus snang bar byas pa/
5a3(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad yongs su smin par bya ba dang /gdul bar brtsom pa'i ye shes mnyam pa nyid kyis snang bar byas pa/chos kyi dbyings kyi snying po gcig tu tha mi dad pa'i ye shes kyi 
5a4(phal chen): snang bar gyur pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa zab mo'i yul yangs pa la ye shes kyi spyod yul gyis snang bar byas pa/sa gcig las byung ba yongs su gnon pa'i rjes su 'brang 
5a5(phal chen): bas shin tu snang bar byas pa/smon lam gyi rgya mtsho la 'jug pa phyi ma'i mtha'i byin gyis rlabs kyi ye shes thams cad kyi gnas pas snang bar gyur pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
5a6(phal chen): gsang ba rmad du byung ba'i dkyil 'khor yangs pa yongs su shes pas snang bar gyur pa/sangs rgya kyi chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid la rjes su brtags pa'i ye shes kyis shin 
5a7(phal chen): tu snang bar gyur pa/de bzhIn gshegs pa'i sa kun tu 'od kyi dbyings dang /gnas su rnam par spyod du dbang ba/ting nge 'dzin rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa'i sgo thams cad du rnam par 
5a8(phal chen): rgyal bas snang bar gyur pa/ye shes snang ba thams cad kyi rjes su song zhing 'gro ba thams cad kyi spyod pa rgya mtsho la shin tu gnas pa/chos rgya mtsho thams cad kyi gzungs dang /
5b1(phal chen): tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa bstan pa dang /yongs su bstan pa la mkhas pa/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya mtshor gtogs pa thams cad kyis khyab par byed pa'i lus can smon lam rgya mtsho mngon 
5b2(phal chen): par bsgrub pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad rgyas par 'gengs pa/sangs rgyas thams cad mchod pa'i sbyor ba la phyi ma'i mur thug pa'i bskul bar dga' ba'i shugs 
5b3(phal chen): kyi snang bar btu thob pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin cing snying po byang chub tu nye bar 'dod pa yang dag par ston pa/kun tu bzang po'i smon lam rgya mtshos sems 
5b4(phal chen): can thams cad ma lus pa thams cad kyi shes pa yongs su rgyas par 'gengs pa'i lus can rnams dang thabs gcig go/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi lag na rdo rje 
5b5(phal chen): dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od dang /lag na rdo rje nyi ma'i shugs kyi rgyal mtshan dang /lag na rdo rje ri rab me tog 'od dang /lag na rdo 
5b6(phal chen): rje rnam dag sprin gyi dbyangs dang /lag na rdo rje dpa' bo shin tu mnga' ba dang /lag na rdo rje rdo rje'i 'od yid du 'ong ba dang /lag na rdo rje shing gi sgra dbyangs dang /lag na rdorje seng seng ge'i 'od kyi 
5b7(phal chen): rgyal po dang /lag na rdo rje 'od 'phro stug pa'i dpal gyi mig dang /lag na rdo rje rin chen gtsug phud padamo'i 'od dang /de dag la stsogs pa lag na rdo rje sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul/shin tu phra ba 
5b8(phal chen): snyed kun kyang /sngon bskal pa mtha' yas par de bzhin gshegs pa mchod par smon pas zham 'bring na 'brang bar gyur pa/sangs rgyas bsnyen bkur ba'i smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin 
6a1(phal chen): @#//pa yongs su rdzogs pa dge ba dang /bsod nams kyi dkyil 'khor mtha' yas pa yongs su dag pa/ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul yangs pa la rnam par spyod pa/rdzu 
6a2(phal chen): 'phrul dang stobs dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul mtha' yas pa khong du chud pa bsam gyi mi khyab pa'i rnam par thar pa'i yul la zhugs pa/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams 
6a3(phal chen): cad kyi zil gyis mi non pa'i lus can sems can rnams ji ltar 'dul ba bzhin du lus shin tu ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa'i sku thams cad kyi 'phrul pa rjes su rtogs pa/sangs 
6a4(phal chen): rgyas thams cad kyi rnam par spyod pa dang /gnas srung ba la shin tu brtson pa rnams dang thabs gcig go/lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba 
6a5(phal chen): snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha khrel dang /dge ba rgyas pa'i 'od dang /'od zer phyogs su snang ba dang /sgras 'dul ba/rgya mtsho'i dpal dang /rgyan bzang po me 
6a6(phal chen): tog gtsug phud dang /spyod yul mtha' yas dpal dang /snang ba'i phreng ba 'dzin pa'i dpal dang /spos dri ma med pa'i sprin las char 'bab pa'i 'od dang /srung 'dzin yongs su 
6a7(phal chen): sdud pa dang /sgo kun nas yongs su gnon pa la mngon du gyur pa'i mig dang khrel dang dpal mi g.yo ba'i 'od dang /lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'ilha de dag la stsogs pa kun 
6a8(phal chen): kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa bsnyen bkur bar smon lam btab pa dang ldan pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdu shin tu phra ba snyed dang thabs gcig go/rkang gis 'gro ba'i lha 
6b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi phyag rgya dang /rkang gis 'gro padamo snang ba'i 'od zer gyi 'od 
6b2(phal chen): dang /rkang gis 'gro me tog gtsug phud kyi dpal dri ma mad pa dang /rkang gis 'gro shin tu mdzes par gnon pa dang /rkang gis 'gro rin chen dbal gyi rgyal mtshan dang /rkang gis 
6b3(phal chen): 'gro dga' ba'i dbyangs snyen pa dang /rkang gis 'phro shing tsan dan gyi 'od dang /rkang gis 'gro bdamo yid du 'ong ba'i 'od dang /rkang gis 'phro rdul gyi shugs kyi 'od dang /rkang gis 
6b4(phal chen): 'gro me tog mang po'i dkyil 'khor dang /de dag la sogs pa rkang gis 'gro ba'i lha/sangs rgyas kyi zhing rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang sngon bskal pa mtha' yas par de bzhin 
6b5(phal chen): gshegs pa'i rjes su 'brang ba sha stag dang thabs gcig go/snying po byang chub kyi lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing rdul shun tu phra ba snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste 'di ltar/snying po byang 
6b6(phal chen): chub kyi lha dge ba dri ma med pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /snying po byang chub kyi lha ri rab spo'i rin po che'i 'od dang /snying po byang chub gyi lha dbyangs kyi 'brug sgra sgrogs 
6b7(phal chen): pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /me tog gi char sna tshogs 'bab pa'i mig dang /'od 'phro mang po shin tu dri ma med pa dang /me tog gi phreng ba 'od kyi gtsug phud dang /rin po che'i rgyan gyi char 
6b8(phal chen): rab tu 'bebs pa dang /mthu dam pa spos kyi mig dang /rdo rje sna tshogs kyi sprin dang /padamo'i shing snang ba'i 'od dang /snying po byangs chub kyi lha dge ba'i lta ba sna tshogs rnam par ston pa'i 
7a1(phal chen): @#//'od dang /de dag la stsogs pa snying po byang chub kyi lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang /sngon sangs rgyas rgya chen mchod pa'i smon lam gyi yang dag par 
7a2(phal chen): 'byung ba sha stag dang thabs gcig go/nam mkha'i lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rngul shin tu phra ba snyed dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar grong khyer gyi lha rin chen rtse mo'i 'od dang /
7a3(phal chen): rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba dang /rin chen dri med shin tu dga' ba dang /smos pa'i mya ngan thams cad spong bas rnam par snang ba dang /sgron ma shin tu rgyas pa'i mig dang /'od 'phro ba'i 
7a4(phal chen): dpal mngon par 'bar ba dang /bsod nams kyi shugs snang ba'i 'od dang /lus dri ma med pas mnyam pa'i 'od dang /spos kyi rgyal mtshan gtsug phud kyi rgyan dang /grong khyer gyi lha rin 
7a5(phal chen): chen dpal gyi 'od dang /de dag la stsos pa nam mkha'i lha/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul/shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang/bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par de bzhin gshegs pa'i gzhal med 
7a6(phal chen): khang rab tu brgyan pa la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/sa'i lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing rdul shin tu phra ba dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun nas shin 
7a7(phal chen): tu rgyas pa dri ma med pa dang /bsod nams brtan pa shin tu bsags pa'i 'ong dang /shing gi yal ga sna tshogs kyi rgyan dang /sems can rin po ches rgyas pa dang /dus dus su dge ba'i mig 
7a8(phal chen): rab tu ston pa dang /gser gyi mig gi dpal dang /spu las spos kyi 'od rnam par 'gyed pa dang /sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong ba sgrog pa dang /me tog gi glong sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /kun nas rdo rje'i 
7b1(phal chen): rdul gyi rgyan dang sa'i lha khyim dri ma med pa'i 'od dang /de dag la stsogs pa sa'i lha sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kun kyang bsod nams kyi dbyabs gcig tu de bzhin gshegs pa'i sngon gyi 
7b2(phal chen): smon lam dang rab tu 'brel pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/ri'i lha tshad med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar ri'i lha rin po che'i rtse mo me tog shin tu rgyas par gnas pa dang /nag tshal sna 
7b3(phal chen): tshogs kyi me tog shin tu rgyas pa'i gtsug phud dang /kun nas shin tu 'phags pas rnam par snang ba'i rgyal mtshan dang /rdul thams cad dang /mi gnas pas dri ma med pa'i gtsug phud dang /snang bas 
7b4(phal chen): phyogs su gsal ba'i 'od dang /stobs la thub pa med pa'i snang ba'i 'od dang /kun nas gzi brjid dpal gyi dkyil 'khor dang /snang ba'i rdul chu bo'i dkyil 'khor dang kun tu mngon par lta ba'i mig 
7b5(phal chen): dang /ri'i lha kun nas sra ba rdo rje'i mtshams kyi mig dang /de dag la sogs pa ri'i lha tshad med pa kun kyang tshul dri ma med pa'i mig gi sgo rab tu thob pa sha stag dang thabs gci ga go/nags tshal gyi lha bsam 
7b6(phal chen): gyis mi khyab pa dag dang yang thabs cig ste/'di ltar nags tshal gyi lha yal ga sna tshogs kyi sprin dang /nags tshal gyi lha 'od kyi phung po sna tshogs kyi mig yid du 'ong ba'i 'od dang /lo ma dri ma med pa'i 
7b7(phal chen): 'phreng ba'i dpal dang /yan lag rab tu 'phyang ba'i gzi brjid dang /dge ba'i 'od sna tshogs dang sgra skad yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs dang /kun nas spos mang po ldang ba'i 'od dang /kun na dpal gyi 'od rab tu 
7b8(phal chen): 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor dang /nags tshal gyi lham me tog dang 'bras bu dang ro'i 'od dang /de dag la sogs ba nags tshal gyi lha bsam gyi mi khyab pa kun kyang dga' ba'i shugs mtha' yas pas gsal bar 
8a1(phal chen): @#//gyur pa sha stags dang thabs gcig go/sman gyi lha mtha' yas pa dag dang ya thabs gcig ste/'di ltar sman gyi lha bun sum tshogs pa dang /tsan dan gyi nags tshal gyi shugs 'chang 
8a2(phal chen): ba dang /'od rdul med pa dang /ming kun tu grags pa glang po'i rgyal mtshan dang /spu'i klong kun nas 'od 'byung ba dang /mun pa nam par sel cing rnam par spyod pa dang /sgra dbyangs kun tu 'byung ba dang /nyi 
8a3(phal chen): ma'i 'od 'gro ba'i mchog gis rgyal mtshan dang /phyogs bcur rmongs pa med par lta ba dang /sman gyi lha mdangs bzang po kun tu 'gyed pa'i mig dang /de dag la sogs pa sman gyi lha mtha' yas pa kun kyang 
8a4(phal chen): snying rje dri ma med pa'i dkyil 'khor gyis rab tu snang bar gyur pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/lo tog gi lha mtha' yas pa dag dngayang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar/lo tog gi lha dang 'jam pa'i dpal dang 
8a5(phal chen): dus kyi me tog rnam par mdzes pa'i 'od dang /rtsal dam pa'i stobs dang mdangs kyi dang mtha' yas pa rab tu 'gyed pa dang /sa kun gyi rtsa ba 'bras bu dang /gtsug phud kha dog sna tshogs dang /yal ga dri ma med par 
8a6(phal chen): grub pa dang /spos sna tshogs shin tu grub pa dang /bzang zhing yid du 'ong bar snang ba dang /lo tog gi lha kha dog rnam par dag cing 'od dri ma med pa dang /de dag la stsogs pa lo tog gi lha mtha' yas ba kun kyang 
8a7(phal chen): dga' ba dang mgu ba dang rangs pa shin tu thob pa sha stag dang thabs gci ga go/chu klung gi lha mu med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di lhar chu klung gi lha chu bo kun dang ldan pa dang /ri dang chu kun la rten pa dang /
8a8(phal chen): rdul thams cad dri ma med par nam par dag pa'i mig dang /de ros khyab par byed pa dang /srog chags kyi rgyud mtha' yas pa skyob pa dang /gdung ba med pa rgya mtsho gdung ba med pa'i 'od dri ma med pa dang /dga' byed 
8b1(phal chen): kun nas 'byung ba dang /yon tan gyi shugs yangs pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /'di gter thams cad kyi dpal rnam par snang ba dang /nyu thung gi lha tshogs chen po rgya mtsho'i dpal gyi 'od dang /de dag la sogs pa chu klung 
8b2(phal chen): gi lha mu med pa kun kyang 'gro ba thams cad la phan pa'i don du yid la byed pa la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/rgya mtsho'i lha bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar rgya mtsho'i 
8b3(phal chen): lha rin po che'i 'od 'byung ba'i dpal dang /rdo rje kun nas sra zhing mi chom sa pa'i rgyal mtshan dang ldan pa dang /rdul dang dri ma med pa'i gnas dang ldan pa dang /kun nas chu dang ri bo dang khyim gyi dkyil 'khor dang /
8b4(phal chen): skyabs rin po che mig gi zla ba'i dpal dang /klu'i dpal pad+mo sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /chun lta bur ro mthun par 'dzin pa'i 'od dang /rin po che'i 'od 'phro ba'i me tog gi tshon gyi 'od dang /ri rab sra ba'i rdo 
8b5(phal chen): rje sna tshogs dang /rgya mtsho'i lha 'brug sgra sgrogs pa rgya mtsho'i shugs kyi dbyangs dang /de dag la sogs pa rgya mtsho'i lha bsam gyis mi bya ba pa kun kyang sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya mtsho'i mthar phyin par 
8b6(phal chen): rgyas par byed pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/me'i lha grangs med pa dang yang thabs gcig ste 'di ltar me'i lha gzi brjid 'bar zhing 'phro ba'i 'od dang /kun tu 'bar zhing 'phro ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi dkyil 
8b7(phal chen): 'khor dang /'od rgya chen pos phyogs rgyas par byed pa dang /rgyal mtshan gyi rdul gyi sgos rgyas par byed pa dang /gzi brjid mi zad pa'i gtsug phud dang /dka' thub sna tshogs kyi mig gi dpal dang /phyogs 
8b8(phal chen): khyim du gyur pa'i ri rab kyi spos dang /'od drag shul can gyi dbang phyug dang /lun pa dang rab rib sel cing phyogs rnam par snang bar byed pa dang /me'i lha 'od 'phro ba'i sprin gyi dbang phyug dang /de dag la sogs 
9a1(phal chen): @#//pa me'i lha grangs med pa kun kyang sems can thams cad kyi gdung ba sel ba'i snang ba ston pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/chu'i phung po'i lha tshad med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig 
9a2(phal chen): ste/'di ltar chu'i phung po'i lha sprin dang na bun kun tu grub pa'i rgyal mtshan dang /sprin sgra'i shugs rgya mtsho'i sprin dang /chu sna tshogs kyi gcug phud dang /bzang por 'khyil ba grub pa'i dpal dang /
9a3(phal chen): kun nas spros dri ma med pa'i myu gu'i dpal dang /dpal rlabs kyi sgra bsod nams stegs kyi 'od dang /tshim par bya ba la dbang byed pa yongs su sgrub pa dang /sa'i rdul snyed kyi sgo nas dbyangs 'byung 
9a4(phal chen): ba dang /'od kun tu 'gro zhing rgyas par byed pa dang /chu'i phung po'i lha rgya mtsho'i dbyangs kyi nga ro'i 'od dang /de dag la sogs pa'i chu'i phung po'i lha tshad med ba kun kyang 'gro ba thams cad la phan pa dang 
9a5(phal chen): bde ba'i skyabs su brtson pa sha stag dang thabs cig go/rlung gi phung po'i lha tshang med pa dag dang yang tha bas gcig ste/'di ltar rlung gi phung po'i lha chags pa med par 'gro ba'i 'od dang /kun tu 
9a6(phal chen): rgyas par 'byung ba'i mthu'i dpal dang /kun tu reg par byed pa'i sprin gyi rgyal mtshan dang /rgyan yid du 'ong zhing bzang ba'i 'od dang /chu drag po'i snying po skems pa dang /skra chen pos 'gro ba'i 
9a7(phal chen): dbyangs dang /shing gi kye mo me tog rab tu 'phyang ba'i gtsug phud dang /thogs pa med par phyogs kun tu rgyas par 'gro ba dang /rlung gi phung po rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba'i sprin gyi dkyil 'khor dang /
9a8(phal chen): rlung gi phung po'i lha 'jig rten thams cad kyi mun pa kun nas snang bar byed pa dang /de dag la sogs pa rlung gi phung po'i lha tshad med pa kun kyang sems can thams cad kyi sngon gyi mtha'i nga rgyal rnam 
9b1(phal chen): bar gtor ba la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/nam mkha'i lha mu med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar nam mkha'i lha kun nas snang ba dri ma med pa'i 'od dang /mtha' yas shing zag pa 
9b2(phal chen): kun tu rgyas par byed pa dang /rlung kun nas 'byung ba'i dpal dang /bsgribs pa thams cad dang mi gnas pa'i dkyil 'khor dang /rgya chen po kun tu gnon pa sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /'od zer snang 
9b3(phal chen): ba'i 'od thogs pa med pa dang /stobs kyi rgyun thogs pa med pa'i dpal dang /phyogs dri ma med par snang ba'i 'od dang /dbyangs shin tu 'phags shing zab pa dang /nam mkha'i lha phyogs bcur mngon du 
9b4(phal chen): ston pa'i 'od dang /de dag la sogs pa nam mkha'i lha mu med pa thams cad kyang sems rgya mtsho dri ma med cing yongs su dag pa sha stag dang thabs gci go/phyogs kyi lha mu med pa dag dang ya thabs 
9b5(phal chen): gcig ste/'di ltar phyogs kyi lha phyogs kyi dbyings na rab tu gnas shing kun tu rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /mnyam pa nyid la mngon du lta ba dang /snang ba spyod pa'i rgyan dang /kun tu rgyu zhing rjes su 
9b6(phal chen): 'brang ba dang /rmongs pa med pa'i gtsug yongs su 'grub pa dang /nam mkha'i rgyud kun tu spyod pa dang /rnam par spyod pa'i sprin rgya chen po'i rgyal mtshan dang /mig tha dad pa med pa'i gtsug phud dang /
9b7(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi bya ba kun nas spyod pa dang /phyogs kyi lha phyogs su rnam par lta ba phreng ba'i 'od dang /de dag la segs pa phyogs kyi lha mu med pa kun kyang /sems can gyi rgyud kyi phyogs snang bar bya 
9b8(phal chen): ba brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/mtshan mo'i lha tshad med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar mtshan mo'i lha dpal dri ma med cing kun nas zab pa'i 'od dang /kun nas skyob pa'i mdang 
10a1(phal chen): @#//kyi dpal dang /rab tu dga' ba'i mig gis 'gro ba la rnam par lta ba dang /sgras zhing rgya mtsho dang ldan pa dang /mchan mo thams cad 'byung ba'i dpal dang /shing thams cad 
10a2(phal chen): rab tu rgyas par byed pa'i 'od dang /kun nas skyong zhing srung ba'i dpal dang /dge ba thams cad kyi dga' ba la dbang sgyur ba dang /mdud par 'dzin pa thams cad zhi bar bya bas dbang po rab tu 
10a3(phal chen): dga' ba dang /mtshan mo'i lha dge ba'i bsod nams 'byung ba'i dpal ston pa dang /de dag la sogs pa mtshan mo'i lha tshad med pa kun kyang chos kyi dga' ba 'byung ba la mos pas gnas pa

10b2(phal chen): yin gyi dbang po rdo rje sra ba sna tshogs kyi yan lag dang /lha ma yin gyi de dbang po stobs rgya chen po'i rgyu'i blo gros dang /lha ma yin gyi da bang po dpal gyi be'u rab tu 'byung ba dang /lha ma yin gyi dad ba 
10b3(phal chen): brtul zhugs bzang po'i dbyangs dang /de dag la sogs pa lha ma yin gyi dbang po kun kyang skyu dang /nga rgyal dang kun nas nyon mongs pa rab tu zhi bar bya ba la brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/
10b4(phal chen): nam mkha' lding gi dbang po bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar nam mkha' lding gi dbang bo mthu stobs chen po'i shugs dang /nam mkha' lding gi dbang mo mi shigs 
10b5(phal chen): pa'i sems can rin chen gtsug phud dang /nam mkha' lding ga dbang po shugs dri ma med pa'i dpal dang /nam mkha' lding dbang po phyir mi ldog pa'i sems kyi rgyan dang /nam mkha' lding 
10b6(phal chen): po rgya mtsho chen po'i 'od kyi tshul 'chang ba dang /nam mkha' lding ge dbang po chos brten pa dri ma med pa'i 'od dang /nam mkha' ldir gi dbang po cod pan sna tshogs dpal gyi gtsug phud dang /namakha' 
10b7(phal chen): lding gi dbang po bzhin kun tu khyab par ston pa'i shugs dang /nam ma kha' lding gi dbang po dge ba rgya mtsho kun nas lta ba dang /nam mkha' ldeng gi dbang po klu'i sgra yangs pa kun tu lta ba'i dkyil

12b1(phal chen): san pa dang /yongs su bsrung bar rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/zla ba'i ris su gtogs pa'i lha du ma dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha'i bu zla ba dang /lha'i bu 'od kyi me tog

13a1(phal chen): @#//gcig ste/'di ltar lha'i dbang po brgya byin dang /lha'i bu sgra kun tu grags pa'i dbang phyug dkyil 'khor dang /byams pa dang lta ba'i gtsug phud dang /rin po che'i 'od kun tu grags pa'i

13a3(phal chen): mig dang /lha'i bu yang dag par bskul ba'i 'od kyi dbang phyug dang /de dag la sogs pa sum cu rtsa gsum gyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha'i bu tshad med pa kun kyang chos rgya chen po'i rang bzhin dang /'gro ba 
13a4(phal chen): thams cad du skyes shing yang dag par bskul ba la rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/rab mtshe ma la sogs pa mtshe ma'i lha'i rgyal po grangs med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar 
13a5(phal chen): lha'i rgyal po rab mtshe ma dang /dga' ba bde ba'i 'od dang /blo gros mi zad pa'i yon tan dpal dang /'byung ba bzang zhing dge ba dang sa dang ri bo'i 'od dang /bsam gyis mi khyab sa pa'i blo gros dang /'khor 
13a6(phal chen): lo'i gtsug dang /gzi brjid 'od 'phro ba dang /'od rnam par snang ba dang /lha'i rgyal po grags pa'i phyogs su rnam par lta ba dang /de dag la sogs ba mtshe ma'i lha'i rgyal po grags med pa kun kyang dka' 
13a7(phal chen): ba dang spro ba rgya chen po mngon par sgrub pa la rab tu brtson pa sha stug dang thabs gcig go/lha'i rgyal po rab dga' ldan la sogs pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag dang yang thabs ga cig ste/'di ltar 
13a8(phal chen): lha'i rgya la po rab dga' ldan dang dga' ba rgya mtsho'i gtsug phud dang /yon tan mchog gi rgyal mtshan dang /'od zer zhi ba'i 'od dang /rab tu dga' ba'i mig dang /rin chen 'od zer zla ba dang /rnam par 
13b1(phal chen): gnon pas rab tu brtson pa dang /rdo rje'i mdog gi 'od bzang po dang /skar mas rgyan pa'i dpal dang /lha'i bu dga' ba bzang po sna tshogs dang /de dag la sogs pa dga' ldan gyi lha'i rgyal po bsam gyis 
13b2(phal chen): mi khyab pa kun kyang /sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu 'byung ba thams cad rjes su dran par yid la bya ba la rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/'phrul dga'i lha'i rgyal po mu med pa dag 
13b3(phal chen): dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha'i rgyal po rab 'phrul dga' dang /sgras zhi ba'i 'od dang /stobs kyi 'phrul ba'i sgra'i 'od dang /dbang chen sna tshogs dang /sprin sgra shin tu 'phags pa dang /snang 
13b4(phal chen): ba'i spyod pa'i dpal dang /gtsug phud yid du 'ong ba'i 'od dang /dga' ba'i blo gros dang /me tog 'od kyi gtsug phud dang /lha'i rgyal po phyogs shin tu snang ba dang /de dag la sogs ba 'phrul dga'i 
13b5(phal chen): lha'i rgyal po mu med pa kun kyang rnam par thar pa rab tu zhi bar sems can thams cad gdul ba la rab tu brtson pa shastag dang thabs gcig go/lha'i rgyal po dbang sgyur la sogs pa bgrang du med pa dag 
13b6(phal chen): dang yang thams gcig ste/'di ltar lha yi rgyal po dbang sgyur dang /lha'i rgyal po mig bzang po'i blo gros dang /cod pan sna tshogs kyi rgyal mtshan dang /brtson pa'i blo gros dang /go 'phang rnam par gnon 
13b7(phal chen): pa'i sgra dbyangs sna tshogs dang /'od yid du 'ong ba'i rgyal mtshan dang /spyod yul zhi ba'i sgo dang /'khor lo'i ri mo bzang po'i rgyal mtshan dang /ma tog blo gros seng+ge'i dbang phyug dang /lha'i rgyal po dbang 
13b8(phal chen): po'i stobs ri mo bzang po'i 'od dang de dag la sogs pa dbang sgyur gyi lha'i rgyal po bgrang du med pa kun kyang /chos kyi dbang rgya chen po la rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/bsam gyis mkhyis 
14a1(phal chen): @#//pa'i tshangs pa chen po dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar tshangs pa chen po rab tu snang ba dang /blo gros kyi 'od dang /blo gros bzang po'i sems snang ba dang /kun tu 
14a2(phal chen): dbyangs kyi sprin dang /'jig rten dbang po'i sgra dang /mig rab tu zhi zhing 'od shin tu zab pa dang /phyogs su snang ba 'gyed pa dang /sprul pa'i sgra dbyangs dang /mig gi 'od zer mngon par 
14a3(phal chen): snang ba dang /tshangs pa chen po dbyangs rgya mtsho yid du 'ong ba dang /de dag la sogs pa tshangs pa chen po bsam gyi mi khyab pa kun kyang /sems can thams cad kyi sems snang bar bya ba 
14a4(phal chen): dang /sim par bya ba la byams pa chen po la gnas pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/lha'i bu gya nom snang ba'i ris su gtogs pa tshad med pa dag dang yang thabs gcigaste/'di ltar 
14a5(phal chen): lha'i bu snang bas dga' ba dang /dge ba sna tshogs kyi 'od dang /dbang po'i dbyangs dang /dran pa'i blo dam pa dang /dga' ba sna tshogs rnam par dag pa'i dbyangs dang /legs par bsam pa'i 
14a6(phal chen): dbyangs dang /'od 'gyed pa'i dbyangs rnam par snang ba dang /dbyangs zab mo'i 'od dang /grag pa dri ma med pa'i 'od dang /lha'i bu 'od dri ma med pas shin tu 'phags pa dang /de dag la sogs ba 
14a7(phal chen): gya nom snang ba'i ris su ga togs pa'i lha'i bu tshad med pa kun kyang zhi ba dga' ba rgya chen po'i rnam par thar pa la gnas pa sha stag dang /thabs gcig go/dge rgyas kyi lha'i bu grangs med pa 
14a8(phal chen): dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di ltar lha'i bu dge bar grags pa'i blo gros dang mchog tu snang ba dang /rab tu zhi ba'i dpal dang /dbyangs kyi ri bo dang /dran pa dge ba'i mig dang /dga' ba 
14b1(phal chen): dam pa rnam par snang ba dang /'jig rten dbyangs kyi blo gros dang /gzi brjid 'bar ba'i bdag po dang /chos kyi dga' bas 'phrul ba'i sems dang /'phrul bas shin tu 'phags pa dang /de dag la sogs pa 
14b2(phal chen): dge rgyas kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha'i bu grangs med pa kun kyang sgo yangs pa la gnas pas 'gro bar 'byung zhing rnam par 'dul ba la rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig go/lha'i bu 'bras bu 
14b3(phal chen): chen po'i ris su gtogs pa tshad med pa dag dang yang thabs gcig ste/'di lta bu lha'i bu chos kyi dpal gzi brjid dga' ba dang /dri med sna tshogs rgya mtsho dang /shes rab mchog gi 'od dang /ye shes 
14b4(phal chen): dbang po'i dpal dang /dga' bzhi pa dang /sgo kun nas shes pa'i mig dang /dga' ba'i gtsug gi blo gros dang /yon tan blo gros bzang po'i 'od dang /'od zhi zhing dri ma med pa dang /lha'i bu snang ba chen 
14b5(phal chen): po dri ma med pa dang /de dag la sog pa 'bras bu chen po'i ris su gtogs ba'i lha'i bu tshad med pa kun kyang zhi ba'i chos kyi gzhal med khang la kun nas rab tu gnas pa sha stag dang thabs gci ga go/
14b6(phal chen): lha'i bu dbang phyug chen po'i ris su gtogasapa grangs med pa dag dang yang tha bas gcig ste/'di ltar lha'i bu shin tu 'od 'phro rgya mtsho dang /lha'i bu de dbang phyug grags pa rab tu snang ba dang /lha'i bu 
14b7(phal chen): dge ba'i mig dri ma med pa dang /lha yi bu shes rab chen pos dga' zhing spro ba dang /lha'i bu 'od mi sgul ba'i dbang phyug dang /lha'i bu mig shin tu mnyam par gzhag pa dang /lha'i bu snang pa shin tu sems 
14b8(phal chen): pa dang /lha'i bu ye shes chen pos dga' ba dang /lha'i bu sna tshogs kun tu dbyangs kyi rgyal mtshan dang /lha'i bu grags pa rnam par snang ba'i brtul zhugs bzang po dang /de dag la sogs pa dbang phyug 
15a1(phal chen): @#//chen po'i ris su gtogs pa'i lha'i bu grang med pa kun kyang spyod yul mtshan ma med pa'i mnyam pa nyid la rab tu brtson pa sha stag dang thabs gcig ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
15a2(phal chen): 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho de ni sems can gyi ris thams cad yang dag par gzhol ba'o//sems can yon tan mtha' yas pa 'dus pa las yongs su byung ba'o//phyogs kyi dbyings kun nas 
15a3(phal chen): shin tu gnas pa'o//'khor gyi dkyil 'khor sna tshogs kyi dbyings su rnam par gnas pa'o//phan tshun 'dres pa med cing ji lta 'ongs pa'i phyogs kyi dbyings la rab tu gnas pa'o//de bzhin gshegs 
15a4(phal chen): pa blta ba la mngon par phyogs pa'o//nyon mongs pa'i bag chags dang /sems kyi dri ma thams cad dang bral ba'o//bsgrib pa thams cad kyi ri rnam par 'thor ba'o//sangs rgyas blta ba la sgrib pa 
15a5(phal chen): med pas snang bar gyur pa ste/de ni 'di ltar bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sngon bskal pa rgya mtsho nas/byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe/bsdu ba'i dngos po 
15a6(phal chen): bzhis yongs su bsdus te/de bzhin gshegs pa de dang de dag gi drung du bzhud pa'i tshe/dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su bzung zhing thabs kyi sgo de dag gis yongs su smin par byas te rnam pa thams cad mkhyen 
15a7(phal chen): pa nyid kyi lam la bkod cing /dge ba'i rtsa ba tshad med pa rab tu bskyed pas bsod nams chen po'i stobs dang shugs rab tu thob pa dag go/smon lam gyi tshul rgya mtsho gzhan dang gzhan la rab tu zhugs 
15a8(phal chen): shing spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor gzhan dang gzhan yongs su dag pas/yang dag par byung ba 'byung ba'i lam gzhan dang gzhan gyis shin tu 'byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa gzhan dang gzhan mthong bas 
15b1(phal chen): snang bar gyur pa dag ste/de dag ni mos pa'i stobs gzhan dang gzhan gyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug go/sangs rgyas blta ba'i rnam par thar pa'i sgo gzhan dang gzhan gyis rnam par 
15b2(phal chen): 'phrul lo//de ni 'di lta ste lha'i bu dbang phyug chen po'i ris su gtogs pa shin tu 'od 'phro rgya mtsho'i rnam par thar pa ni chos kyi dbyings dang /nam mkha' ltar zhi ba'i tshul gyi mthu zhes bya'o//lha'i bu 
15b3(phal chen): dbang phyug grags pa rab tu snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni chosathams cad la dbang ba'i stobs la yang dag par gzhol zhing kun nas rjes su 'gro bas rnam par spyod pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu dge ba'i mig dri ma 
15b4(phal chen): med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos thams cad 'byung ba med pa dang /'gro ba med pa dang /brjod du med cing sbyar ba med pa zhes bya'o//lha'i ru shes rab chen pos dga' zhing spro ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos 
15b5(phal chen): kyi tshul thams cad la mngon sum du gyur pa'i shes rab rgya mtshos rnam par dpyod pa la rab tu snang ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu 'od mi sgul ba'i dbang phyug gi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can la bsam gtan 
15b6(phal chen): gyi dga' ba dang bde ba mtha' yas pa la mos pa bstan pa/rgya chen po'i tshul yang dag par 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu mig shin tu mnyam par gzhag pa'i rnam par thar ba ni/mi shes pa'i mun nag

15b8(phal chen): 'jug pa zhes bya'o//lha'i bu ye shes chen pos dga' ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos thams cad phyi phyogs su 'gro zhing yongs su gnon pa dang /brten pa'i phyogs kyi mthu zhes bya'o//lha'i bu sna tshogs 
16a1(phal chen): @#//kun tu dbyangs kyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yul rab tu zhi ba la 'jug pa/kun nas khong du chud pa'i gtsug rab tu snang ba zhes bya'o//lha'i bu grags pa

16a3(phal chen): dbang phyug chen po'i ris su gtogs pa'i lha/shin tu 'od 'phro rgya mtshos sangs rgyas kyi mthus lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su

16a5(phal chen): skyes shing byung //chos kyi sgron ma sangs rgyas shin tu ston//chos la mnga' rab mdzad pa 'jig rten byung //de yi spyod yul mtha' yas mi zad de//dbang phyug grag pa'i 'di 'dra'i chos la 'jug 
16a6(phal chen): bsam gyis mi khyab sangs rgyas snang ba med//de yi mtshan nyid phyogs na med pa ste//rnam dag la ma ni skye bo dag la ston//shin tu dri med mig gis 'di 'dra mthong //'gro ba kun gyis bsam 
16a7(phal chen): du mi nus ste//rgyal ba'i shes rab mtha' yas gang mnga' ba//gti mug bsgribs pa'i sems can rnam par sbyong //de la shes rab chen po rnam par gnas//skye dgu kun gyis sangs rgyas

16b1(phal chen): la//sangs rgyas chos nyid rab tu zhi ba ston//shes rab sgron ma 'gro ba 'di brjod do//dge ba'i mig ni 'di yi tshul la 'jug/sangs rgyas kun kyang rnam dag sku sten cing //thams cad phyogs su shin
