44a2(phal chen): mkha'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba'i spyan ni rgya chen yangs pa yis//sems can rgya mtsho ma lus kun gzigs 
44a3(phal chen): te//'gro ba kun la mngon par rab tu bzhugs//sangs rgyas dri med nam mkha' rab tu 'dra//sangs rgyas sku yi rgya chen 'od kyi rnams//phyogs bcu'i 'gro la khyab cing rnam par mdzes//thams cad 
44a4(phal chen): phyogs su mngon par rab tu gnas//kun tu rgyas pa'i 'od kyis 'di mthong ngo //rgyal ba nam mkha'i mtshan nyid 'byung ba med//'di la gzung ba snang ba gang yang med//thob pa med cing rang bzhin med 
44a5(phal chen): pa ste//rlung gi 'od kyis rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //bskal pa rgya mtsho brgya phrag mang por yang //sangs rgyas sems can bsgribs pa sel bar mdzad//lam yangs rnam par thar pa rab tu ston//'di ni 
44a6(phal chen): dpa' bo dkyil 'khor 'od kyi'o//sngon cad sangs rgyas byang chub spyod pa'i rnams//'gro ba kun la phan dang bde don du//ji ltar byung ba de dag bdag gis mthong //'di ni sna tshogs gtsug phud spyod yul lo//
44a7(phal chen): 'khor ba rgya mtsho kun tu rgyu ba yi//sems can log par ltung zhing 'khor ba la//sangs rgyas 'od ni sdug bsngal zhi ba'i rgyu//snang ba'i 'od kyis rnam thar 'di ma thong ngo //bsod nams dge ba thams cad snying 
44a8(phal chen): po ste//chags med stobs dang ye shes rgya chen mkhyen//'gro ba thams cad bsod nams zhing gyur pa//sangs rgyas de ltar chags med stobs sems so//mun pa stug po spyod yul rnam rgyu ba'i//sems 
44b1(phal chen): can bsgribs pas rab tu slob pa la//de bzhin gshegs pa snang ba rab 'gyed de//dri med phyogs snang lhas ni de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas ye shes shin tu 'phags pa yi//mtha' dang dbus yang dmigs 
44b2(phal chen): pas mi rung ste//zhing kun 'gro la mngon par 'od kyang snang //zab mo'i 'od kyis de ltar sangs rgyas mthong //phyogs rnams rgya mtsho kun na spyod pa yis//sems can thams cad yongs su 'grol ba'i phyir//
44b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas smon lam rgya chen shin tu yang //mngon par lta bas rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //de la rlung gis phung po'i lha chags pa med par 'gro ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni//'jig rten gyi bya ba thams cad sangs rgyas 
44b4(phal chen): kyi chos su yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha kun tu rgyas par 'byung ba'i mthu'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas mchod pa'i yul mtha' yas pa rgya chen po mngon par sgrub pa ston pa zhes bya'o//rlung 
44b5(phal chen): gi lha kun tu rig par byed pa'i sprin gyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi nang rab tu zhi bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lta rgyan yid du 'ong zhing bzang po'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/'gro ba 
44b6(phal chen): thams cad kyi bsgribs pa'i ri 'joms pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha chu drag po'i snying po skems pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/bdud kyi 'khor lo sgo kun nas 'joms pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi 
44b7(phal chen): lha sgra chen pos 'gro ba'i dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sem can mtha' yas pa'i 'jigs pa rnam par zlog cing zhi bar byed pa zhes bya'o////rlung gi lha shing gi rtse mo'i me tog rab tu 'phyang ba'i gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar 
44b8(phal chen): pa ni/chos thams cad kyi tshul tha dad pa med par yang dag par shes pa rgya mtsho la 'jug pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha thogs pa med par phyogs kun tu rgyas par 'gro ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad mnyam par 
45a1(phal chen): @#//dgod pa'i tshul gyis 'dul ba'i snying po zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha rlung gi phung po rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba'i sprin gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/bsam gtan gyi sgo rab tu zhi ba'i 
45a2(phal chen): shugs kyi gti mug rab tu zhi bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rlung gi lha 'jig rten thams cad kyi mun pa kun nas snang bar byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa'i rjes su snyegs pas thogs pa 
45a3(phal chen): med pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//de nas rlung gi lha chags pa med par 'gro ba'i 'od kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthus/rlung gi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//
45a4(phal chen): rgyal ba kun gyi chos nyid zab mo 'dzin pa ste//chags med thams cad tshul gyi de la 'jug/'gro ba kun la sangs rgyas mngon tu bzhugs//de ni mtshan med gzugs brnyan yod pa min//sangs rgyas thams cad 
45a5(phal chen): mgul rnam par ltos//byang chub spyad pa mtha' yas spyod pa'i tshe//skad cig gcig la sangs rgyas mchod cing bstod//de la rgyas byed 'od kyi lha 'jug go/sangs rgyas bsam gyis mi khyab 'gro ba'i mgon//tshul rnams 
45a6(phal chen): kun gyi sbyor ba chu dang me//sdug bsngal kun las sems can ma lus skyob//rgyal mtshan reg pas rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //'ge bas dang sgribs pa shin tu stug po yi//mi des bsgribs shing bsod nams med pa yi//
45a7(phal chen): sems can sdug bsngal kun las dgrol bar 'gyur//sman gyis brgyan pa'i lhas ni 'di mthong ngo //rgyal ba'i rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul la ltos//rnam pa kun gyis bdud rnams shin tu 'joms//tshul gyis 'dul 
45a8(phal chen): bde ba rab tu sgrogs//gzi brjid drag po'i mthus ni 'di mthong ngo //rgyas pa'i ba spu'i sgo rnams thams cad nas//'gro ba kun 'gangs sgra yi dbyangs 'byung ba//de dag sdug bsngal 'jig pa thams cad med//
45b1(phal chen): 'di ni sgra sgrogs lha yis rab tu shes//'gro ba kun mnyam rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams su//mdo las dag mkhyen pas bde bar gshegs//mtha' yas bskal par rang byung yang dag ston//shing gi gtsug phud 'byung ba de la 
45b2(phal chen): 'jug/rgyal ba'i ye shes thabs rnams kun sgo nas//kun tu chags shing thogs pa med pa ste//spyod yul gzhan yang kun tu mtha' yas med//'di ni chags med 'gro ba'i rnam thar te//rgyal ba'i spyod yul mtha' 
45b3(phal chen): yas dag la ltos//rab zhi sku mnga' mtshan med sangs rgyas te//thabs sgo kun nas 'od kyang rab tu snang //phung po sna tshogs pa dag po'i rnam thar to//mi mngon bskal pa mtha' yas spyod pa'i tshe//de 'dra'i chos kyis 'gro 
45b4(phal chen): kun tshim mdzad pas//thams cad stobs la sangs rgyas rab tu bzhugs//'jig rten stobs 'dzin lha yis 'di mthong ngo //de la me'i lha gzi brjid 'bar zhing 'gro ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon 
45b5(phal chen): mongs pa'i dpa' rlabs dang thams cad dang mun pa thams cad dang mi gnas pa zhes bya'o//me'i lha kun tu 'bar zhing 'gro ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sem can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i dpa' 
45b6(phal chen): rlabs dang rab tu gdung ba rnam par zlog pa zhes bya'o//me'i lha 'od rgya chen pos phyogs rgyas par byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/bsod nams kyi stobs mi skul ba dang /snying rje ba dang /byams pa'i snying po zhes bya'o//me'i lha 
45b7(phal chen): rgyal mtshan gyi rdul gyi sgos rgyas par byed pa'i rnam thar pa ni/nyon mongs pa'i rdul gyis g.yog pa rnam par sel ba zhes bya'o//me'i lha gzi brjid mi zad pa'i gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/nam mkha' mtha' yas pa 
45b8(phal chen): snang bas gsal bar byed pa'i 'od ces bya'o//me'i lha dka' thub sna tshogs kyi mig dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/dge ba dang /bsod nams sna tshogs rnam par snang ba'i 'dor ba tu zhi ba zhes bya'o//me'i lha phyogs kyis 
46a1(phal chen): @#//
46b1(phal chen): sla ba min//rnam thar 'di ni gzi brjid gtsug phud kyi'o//phyogs bcu'i 'khor rnams shin tu yang dag par//sangs rgyas mgon po thams cad mngon du lta//'gro ba kun la thub pa rnam par ma rdos//'di la bka' thub 
46b2(phal chen): 'od 'phro dpal 'jug go/'gro ba kun na thub pa rab tu 'phags//khang khyim thams cad dag na bzhugs par snang //rnam par yangs pa'i chos kyi char yang 'bebs//'di ni phyogs snang byed pa'i spyod yul lo//rgyal ba 
46b3(phal chen): nyid kyi chos rnams zab pa ste//thams cad chos kyi 'gro bar rang byung zhing //ngo bo nyid kyi chos nyid ston pa mdzad//de la drag shul 'od ni rab tu dga'//lta ba'i mun nag gti mug sgrib pa yi//'jig rten bdag med 
46b4(phal chen): 'khrugs pa thams cad la//rgyal bas chos ni rab tu bstan mdzad ste//de la rnam par snang ba'i lha dga' 'o//rgyal ba'i smon lam sngon cad byung ba'i rnams//ci 'dra bsam gyis khyab par mi rung ste//stobs dang pha rol 
46b5(phal chen): phyin pas rnam par sbyang //'od 'phro sprin ni 'di la 'di ltar 'jug/de la chu'i lha sprin dang na bun kun tu grub pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad la byams pa'i tshul la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes 
46b6(phal chen): bya'o//chu'i lha sprin sgra'i shugs rgya mtsho'i sprin gyi rnam par thar pa ni/chos kyi sprin mtha' yas pa 'byung ba'i rgyan ces bya'o//chu'i lha chu sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba snod du gyur pa thams cad 
46b7(phal chen): la rnam par lta zhing /sdud pa'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//chu'i lha bzang por khyim pa grub pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yul zab mo'i 'od kyi char pa'i byin gyi rlabs shes bya'o//chu'i lha kun nas spos dri ma med 
46b8(phal chen): pa'i myu gu'i dpal gyi rnam par thar ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi 'od dri ma med pa rnam par lta ba la yang dag par 'jug pa'i byin gyi brlabs shes bya'o//chu'i lha dpa' rlabs kyi sgra bsod nams stegs kyi 'od kyi rnam par thar 
47a1(phal chen): pa ni/chos kyi dbyings mtshan nyid med cing dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//chu'i lha tshim par bya ba la dbang byed pa yongs su sgrub pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/snying rje rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i sgor 'jug pa zhes bya'o//
47a2(phal chen): chu'i lha sa'i rdul snyed kyi sgo nas dbyangs 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du dga' ba'i shugs 'byung ba'i snying po zhes bya'o//chu'i lha 'od kun du 'gro 
47a3(phal chen): zhing rgyas par byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni bsod nams chags pa med pa shin tu rgyas par rab tu snang bar byed pa'i dkyil 'khor ces bya'o//chu'i lha rgya mtsho'i dbyings kyi nga ro'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro 
47a4(phal chen): ba thams cad 'dul ba'i sbyor ba nam mkha' lta bus 'dul bar spyod pa zhes bya'o//de nas chu'i lha sprin dang na bun kun tu grub pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/chu'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams 
47a5(phal chen): cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba'i mtshan rnams re re thams cad kyang //sangs rgyas zhing gi rdul dang mnyam pa yi//byams pa mngon du gyur pas byung ba bzang //
47a6(phal chen): sems can kun gyis bltas zhing mi ngoms so//'gro ba kun tu rgyal ba rnams drung du//skyo ba med par spyad cing rnams sbyangs te//rnam pa sna tshogs chos kyi sgo yang ston//rgya mtsho shugs ni 'di'i tshul la 'jug/
47a7(phal chen): rgyal ba rnams ni 'pho bar mi snang ste//phyogs rnams bcu nas byon par snang ba med//snod kyi tshul bzhin sems can rnams kyang ste//chu'i gtsug phud dkyil 'khor da la lta//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul 
47a8(phal chen): ni gzhal du med//sems can kun gyis bsam par mi nus te//phyogs rnams kun tu khyab par sgra sgrog go/'di ni snyan pa'i gtsug phud yul yin no//sangs rgyas 'od ni mi zad gnas pa ste//bsam gyis 
47b1(phal chen): mi khyab chos kyi dbyings kyis kyang //sems can thams cad 'dul ba rab tu ston//spos kyi phung po sna tshogs lha yis mthong //de bzhin gshegs pa nam mkha' mnyam pa ste//phyogs rnams kun tu dri med mtshan nyid med//
47b2(phal chen): phyogs rnams kun na'ang bzhugs par rab tu snang //dpa' rlabs sgra'i bsod nams 'od kyis mthong //snying rje'i sgo ni rgya mtsho lta bu ste//sems can kun dang mnyam par sngon bshad do//sprin dang 'dra bar 'gro bar mngon sum mo//
47b3(phal chen): de ltar de nas dga' ba 'byung bas mthong //phyogs bcu zhing rnams ci snyed gang yod pa//de dag kun na sangs rgyas gdan bzhugs snang //shin tu rgyas pa'i byang chub mngon sangs rgyas//dga' ba rdul med dbyangs kyi de mthong ngo //
47b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas spyod yul chags pa med pa ste//shin tu rgya che phyogs rnams kun tu gnas//rnam par 'phrul pa rgyal ba ston mdzad do//de la 'phro ba'i 'od kyi lha 'jug go/sbyor ba'i spyod pa rnams la mtha' med de//'gro ba kun dbyings 
47b5(phal chen): thub pa med par 'jug/rgyal ba'i byin gyi rlabs ni 'di 'dra ste//nga ros 'gengs pa rnam thar 'di la 'jug/de la rgya mtsho'i lha rin po che'i 'od 'byung ba'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni//'gro ba thams cad dang mnyam pa'i bsod nams 
47b6(phal chen): rgya mtsho rin po che 'byung ba'i rgyan ces bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha rdo rje kun nas sra zhing mi choms pa'i rgyal mtshan dang ldan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad bsrung ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha rdul dang dri ma med pa'i gnas 
47b7(phal chen): dang ldan pa'i rnam par tharapa ni//sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa rgya mtsho mang po rnam par sel ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lta kun nas chu dang ri bo dang khyim gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi ngan song dang 
47b8(phal chen): ngan 'gro'i lam rnam par zlog pa zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha skyabs rin po che mig gi zla ba'i dpal gyi rnam par thang pa ni//shugs chen pos gti mug rnam par sel ba zhes bya 'o//rgya mtsho'i lha klu'i dpal pad mo sna tshogs kyi gtsug 
48a1(phal chen): @#//phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/rgyud thams cad kyi sdug bsngal rab tu zhi bar byed cing bde ba 'byung bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha chu lta bur ro mthun par 'dzin pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni sems can 
48a2(phal chen): thams cad kyi lta ba dang /rmongs pa'i rang bzhin dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho lha rin po che 'od 'phro ba'i me tog gi mtshon gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/rin po che'i rigs thams cad 'byung zhing /byang chub kyi sems 
48a3(phal chen): rab tu skye ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lha'i rab sra ba'i rdo rje sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems rgya mtsho mi sgrub pa'i bsod nams la rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//rgya mtsho'i lta 'brug sgra sgrog pa rgya mtsho'i shugs kyi dbyangs 
48a4(phal chen): kyi rnam par thar pa ni/ting nge 'dzin gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad chos kyi dbyings su yang dag par 'jug pa'i rgyal mtshan ces bya'o//de nas rgya mtsho'i lha rin po che'i 'od 'byung ba'i dpal gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/rgya 
48a5(phal chen): mtsho'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cadal rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bskal pa rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab par//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho rgya chen mang mchod cing //'gro ba 
48a6(phal chen): dag la bsod nams mtsho spel bas//de phyir rgyal ba lhan me lhang nge'o//'gro ba kun tu rgyal ba mngon par mdzes//sems can kun gyi dbang po bsam rgyud du//chos kyi rgya mtsho rgya chen ston pa mdzad//rdo rje sems dpa' 
48a7(phal chen): sra ba'i dpal dga' bo//'gro ba kun tu 'jig rten mgon po ni//chos kyi sprin las char chen yangs pa 'bebs//sdug bsngal rgya mtsho zhi mdzad thad mi shes//rdul dang dri med 'di'i tshul la 'jug/sems can 
48a8(phal chen): thams cad mnyam med nyon mongs bsgribs//lam rgyud rgya mtsho'i sdug bsngal nang na 'khor//de dag la ni rgyal ba'i yul ston te//dpal bcu'i lha ni 'di la rab tu dga'//bskal pa rgya mtsho mang po bsam las su//
48b1(phal chen): rgyal bas spyod pa mi zad rnams spyad de//'gro ba'i gti mug dkyil 'khor rnam par zlog/rin chen zla ba'i dpal ni 'di la 'jug/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho sdug bsngal chen po na//sems can 'khor zhing mi bde kun gzigs na//
48b2(phal chen): rgyal bas bla na med pa'i lam yang bstan//klu dpal pad+mo gtsug phud 'di la dga'//sangs rgyas spyod yul bsam gyis mi khyab ltos//chos kyi dbyings dang nam mkha'i mtshan nyid de//'gro ba'i gti mugs dra ba rnam par sbyong //de skad 
48b3(phal chen): chu 'dzin shugs ni smra zhing brjod//sangs rgyas dri med bsam gyis mi khyab spyad//'jig rten kun gyi yul du rnam par mdzes//spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor 'gro ba kun la ston//sna tshogs dpal gyi me tog spyod yul lo//bdud 
48b4(phal chen): kyi dkyil 'khor mi zad rgya che ba'ang //'byam klas thugs kyi skad cig gcig gi brtul//rgyal ba bsam gyis mi khyab mi g.yo' ste//rdo rje rdul 'dzin tshul ni 'di yin no//chos kyi dbyings ni 'byam klas kun bkang zhing //
48b5(phal chen): phyogs bcu rnams su rgyal ba'i gsung yang grag/sangs rgyas spyod yul ting nge 'dzin las skyes//'di ltar 'di la sprin 'byung rab tu 'jug/de la chu klung gi lha chu bo kun dang ldan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos kyi chu bo mtha' 
48b6(phal chen): med pas rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lha ri dang chu kun la rten pa'i rnam par thar pa na//nyon mongs pa thams cad spang ba la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//chu klung ga lha rdul thams cad dri ma med par rnam par 
48b7(phal chen): dag par dag pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i rdul dang 'grog pa'i snying rje'i tshul gyi kun tu rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes pa'o//chu klung gi lha nga ros khyab par byed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni 
48b8(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi sgra yid du mi 'ong ba zlog cing phan bskyed pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gis lha srog chags kyi rgyud mtha' yas pa skyob pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad la 'tshe ba med pa'i byams pa 'byung 
49a1(phal chen): @#//bzhes bya'o//chu klung gi lha gdung pa med pa rgya mtsho gdung ba med pa'i 'od dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad gdung bar mi nus pa'i sgo kun nas ston pa zhes bya'o//chu 
49a2(phal chen): klung gi lha dga' bde kun nas 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi 'byung ba sel cing gtong ba phun sum tshogs pa yongs su spyod pa zhes bya'o//chu klung gi lha yon tan gyi shugs yangs pa'i rgyal 
49a3(phal chen): mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/thams cad tshims par byed pa'i bsod nams kyi zhing rab tu 'byung ba zhes bya'o//chu klung gis lha 'jig rten thams cad kyi dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sem can thams cad kyi nyon 
49a4(phal chen): mongs pa rnam par byang bar byed cing /'khon du 'dzin pa dam po dang /khro ba'i shugs dang bar byed pa zhes bya'o//chu klung ga la lha tshogs chen po rgya mtsho dpal gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad bde ba phun 
49a5(phal chen): sum tshogs pa myong bar byed cing thar par rgya mtsho la 'dzud pa zhes bya'o//de nas chu klung gis lha chu bo kun dang ldan pas sangs rgyas kyi mthus/chu klung gi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te/
49a6(phal chen): de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//chos rnams rgya mtsho mtha' yas spyod pa dag/thams cad rgyal bas sems can don du sbyangs//'jig rten bkang zhing nyon mongs gdungs pa'i rnams//sprin chen cher bzhin 
49a7(phal chen): rab tu zhi bar mdzad//bskal pa brjod du med par rgyal bas sngon//smon lam 'od kyis sems can dbyings shes shing //'gro ba yongs smin dbang po chos kyang mkhyen//'khyil pa zhes bya chu klung lha dga' bo//'gro dang mnyam 
49a8(phal chen): pa'i snying rje'i tshul bsgoms shing //mgon po sems can nyon mongs dri ma sbyong //yongs smin sems can rgyal ba mngon du lta//de la dri med mig gi lha yang mos//de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs snyan rgya chen ni/kun tu 
49b1(phal chen): bsgrags pas 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal zhi//sangs rgyas dbyangs snyan thos nas dga' mgu yid//rnam thar 'di ni sgra dbyangs sgrogs pas thos//bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro ba phan don du//sngon cad byang chub spyod pa spyad mthong na//de 
49b2(phal chen): phyir de ni phyogs bkang 'jig rten mdzes//lam rgyud stobs pa rjes su dran zhing dga'//rgyal ba sngon cad spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe//sna tshogs sgo nas sems can smin par mdzad//de dag bsod nams rgya mtshos sdug bsngal 'jil//zhi byed 
49b3(phal chen): chu klung lha ni des dga' bo//sbyin pa'i sgom tshad med mi zad mang //'gre ba 'dod pa kun la yongs btang zhing //rgyal bas 'gro ba'i 'jungs pa yongs su bstsal//kun nas dga' dpal rnam thar 'di mthong ngo //thabs tshul rgya mtsho thams 
49b4(phal chen): cad bsgoms pas na//yon tan mang po brgya phrag mang pos byung //rgyal bas 'gro ba thams cad tshim mdzad de//rgya chen sgra dbyangs shugs ldan lha yang dga'//sgribs pa'i glong dang 'gro ba'i nyon mongs dri//'gro ba rnams la byams 
49b5(phal chen): pa bskyed sbyangs pas//mkha' mnyam 'od kyis sangs rgyas rnam par mdzes//rnam par mdzes pa'i chu klung lha dga' bo//sangs rgyas rang byung yon tan rgya mtsho ste//bsod nams zhing ni 'dus byas sdug bsngal 'dor//byang chub 
49b6(phal chen): lam la rim gyi yong smin mdzad//rgya mtsho'i tshogs dpal rnam thar 'di yin no//de la yo tog gi lha dang 'jam pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni//ro dang mdangs thams cad skyed cing //sems can thams cad sangs rgyas kyi gzugs 
49b7(phal chen): kyi sku rab tu thob pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lha dus kyi me tog rnam par mdzes pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad nye bar 'tsho bas dga' ba chen po'i shugs kyi tshim pa skyed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gis lha bstsal dam pa'i 
49b8(phal chen): stobs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/chos thams cad kyi dbyings yongs su rgyas pas shin tu dag pa'i spyod yul zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lham dangs kyi dang mtha' yas pa rab tu 'gyed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/snying rje mtha' yas pa'i sgo nas 
50a1(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul bar rab tu 'gyed cing rjes su dran pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gi lha kun gyi rtsa ba 'bras bu'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad mngon du lta zhing /sangs rgyas 
50a2(phal chen): rnams la yon tan yang dag par bskyed pa ston pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gyi lha gtsug phud kha dog sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi mos pa'i me tog yongs su sbyangs pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gis lha yal 
50a3(phal chen): ga dri ma med par grub pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can la phan pa'i byams pa dang /bsod nams rgya mtsho rgya chen po rnam par 'phel bar byed pa zhes bya'o//lo tog gilha spos sna tshogs shin tu grub pa'i rnam par 
50a4(phal chen): thar pa ni/chos thams cad la spyod pa kun tu gnon pas rtsom pa zhes bya'o//lo tog ga lha bzang zhing yid du 'ong bar snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gre ba thams cad kyi zhum pa dang /yid du mi 'ong ba rnam par zlog cing /
50a5(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings thams cad yongs su dag par byed pa zhes bya'o//lo tag gi lha kha dog rnam par dag cing /'od dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba la rnam par lta zhing chos kyi 'khor gyi 
50a6(phal chen): dbang po tshim par byed pa zhes bya'o/de nas lo tog gi lha dang 'jam dpal gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/lo tog gi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag 
50a7(phal chen): smras so//rang byung ye shes sgron ma skyod pa ni//'gro ba kun gyi skyabs dang mgon gyur te//ma lus 'gro ba'i bde ba yang dag skyed//bsod nams rgya mtsho sangs rgyas bla na med//sangs rgyas mnyan na don yod 
50a8(phal chen): yon tan sprin//'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal zhi bar mdzad//chos rgya chen po mi zad rab tu bskyed//dus kyi dkyil 'khor lha ni 'di la 'jug/bde gshegs stobs ni ma lus yongs su rgyas//'gro ba dag 
50b1(phal chen): ni chos kyi legs par mdzes//'gro ba kun 'dul kun tu rnam par snang //stobs dang gzugs kyi mthu yis de mthong ngo//sems kyi skad cig kun la 'gro mnyam pa'i//thugs rjes rgya mtsho ldan cad rnam sbyangs pas//de phyir 
50b2(phal chen): rdzu 'phrul rnams ni mtha' yas so//de ni mdangs mthong lha yis rab tu mthong //thabs rnams kun gyi sgo rnams don yod par//sangs rgyas 'jig rten kun tu khyab gyur cing //'gro ba kun gyi nyon mongs dag par mdzad//
50b3(phal chen): 'di ni thams cad sgo mo'i rnam thar to//'gro ba 'di na mos yangs gang yod pa//de dag thams cad rgyal ba'i mthus byung ste//de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes rgya mtsho'o//nag tshal rgyan gyis gtsug phud de la 'jug 
50b4(phal chen): 'jig rten rnams la byams pa che bskyed de//sems can rnams la phan phyir rang byung skyes//lam rab dge ba zhi ba rab tu ston//zhi ba sna tshogs dpal gyi rnam thar to//phyogs su sems can yongs su smin byed 
50b5(phal chen): pa'i//bde gshegs spyod pa ji ltar rnam dag pa//byang chub shing las de dag sgra yang 'byung //spos zhim dpal gyi lha yis de thos so//rgyal ba'i 'jig rten yid du 'ong ba spong //dga' ba ma lus rnams kyang 
50b6(phal chen): rab tu skyed//'gro ba kun gyi dbang po rnam par sbyong //dge bar snang ba'i lha yis de mthong ngo//'gro ba kun na sangs rgyas shin tu 'phags//skye bo kun gyis bsam pa de mkhyen to//sna tshogs sgo nas 'gro ba 
50b7(phal chen): yongs smin mdzad//rnam dag kha dog 'od kyi rnam thar to//de la sman gyi lha phun sum tshogs pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/bsam pa kun nas rnam par lta ba'i gnon pas 'gro ba rab tu gnon pa zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha 
50b8(phal chen): tsan dan gyi nags tshal gyi shugs 'chang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/lta ba yod pas sems can 'dren pa'i 'od ces bya'o//sman gyi lha 'od rdul med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad nyon mongs par bcu zhi bas rdul 
51a1(phal chen): @#//dang bral ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha ming kun tu grags pa'i glang po'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/ming bsgrags pas dag pa'i rtsa ba rgya mtsho yang dag par 'phel ba zhes bya'o//
51a2(phal chen): sman gyi lha spu'i klong kun nas 'od byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/nad thams cad la snying rje'i dkyil 'khor gyi shugs kyi rgyal mtshan zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha mun pa rnam par sel cing rnam par sbyong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni 'gro ba 
51a3(phal chen): mdongs pa lta bur thams cad kyi ye shes kyi mig rnam par dag par byed pa zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha sgra dbyangs kun tu 'byung ba'i rnam par thar ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos rab tu bsgrags pas don gyi dbyings 'byung ba zhes bya'o//
51a4(phal chen): sman gyi nyi ma'i 'od 'gro ba'i mchog gi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thal ba ni/'gro ba thams cad la dge ba'i bshes su ston cing dge ba'i rtsa bas yang dag par bskul ba zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha phyogs bcur rmongs pa med pa'i rnam 
51a5(phal chen): par thar pa ni/mos pa'i tshul gyis rnam par dag pa'i snying rje'i snying po zhes bya'o//sman gyi lha mdangs bzang po kun tu 'gyed pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi nad rab tu zhi bar byed cing /sangs rgyas rjes 
51a6(phal chen): su dran par sbyong ba zhes bya'o//de nas sman gyi sa lha phun sum tshogs pas sangs rgyas kyi mthus/sman gyi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs 
51a7(phal chen): rgyas ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab ltos//'gro ba kun gyi bsam pa rab tu mkhyen//thabs kyi rnam pa sna tshogs sgo dag nas//sdug bsngal kun las sems can rab tu 'grol//sangs rgyas chos nyid de ltar bsam mi khyab//
51a8(phal chen): de yi mthu ni thams cad don kyang yod//skye dgu rnams kyi sdug bsngal zhi bar mdzad//tsan dan shugs dang ldan pa'i rnam thar ro//sngon cad rgyal ba'i chos nyid dag la ltos//bskal pa rgya mtsho mtha' med spyod pa na//srid pa kun 
51b1(phal chen): la chags pa gang yang med//de la rdul med 'od kyi lha 'jug go/rgyal ba bskal pa stong phrag brgya dag tu//mthong zhing mtshan nyid de bzhin shin tu dkon//tshul rnams kun tu rang byung don yod de//de la rgyal mtshan 
51b2(phal chen): mchog ni de ltar 'jug/rgyal ba'i spu yi sgo rnams kun nas kyang //nad las rab tu grol ba'i 'od bkye bas//'jig rten nyon mongs ma lus zhi bar mdzad//spu mdangs rgyun gyi rnam thar 'di yin no//sdug bsngal las dang 
51b3(phal chen): nyon mongs sna tshogs pas//'gro ba mdongs shing rab tu rmengs gyur rnams//sangs rgyas ye shes 'od kyi rnam par spyod//de ni mun dang bral ba'i lhas mthong ngo //rgyal ba'i gsungs gcig las ni mtha' yas pa'i//chos kyi sgo 
51b4(phal chen): mo thams cad kun kyang 'byung //de ni sems can ma lus kun kyang shes//'di ni sgrib dang ldan pa'i rnam thar to//rgyal ba'i ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab ltos//srid par sems can yongs su smin byas pa//de la 'gro kun 
51b5(phal chen): mi mgon mngon du snang //nyi 'od zhi ba'i lha yis de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas thugs rje'i tshul ni rgya mtsho'i ste//'gro ba kun la phan don de skyes so//'gro ba kun la lam rnams shin tu ston//de ni phyogs su snang ba'i lhas shes 
51b6(phal chen): so//sangs rgyas 'gro dbang rnams kyi 'od yangs pa//phyogs rnams thams cad ma lus kun tu gang //sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i gzi brjid bskyed//mdangs kyi 'od du ldan pa'i rnam thar to//sangs rgyas rmad ga cad 
51b7(phal chen): ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////bam po bzhi pa////de lan gas tshal gyi lha yal ga sna tshogs kyi sprin gyi rnam par thar pa ni//ye shes rgya mtsho'i yangs shing mtha' yas pa'i snying po zhes bya'o//nags 
51b8(phal chen): tshal gyi lha 'od kyi phung po sna tshogs kyi mig yid du 'ong ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/dge ba rgya chen po kun nas bsrungs pa'i shugs zhes bya'o//nags tshal gyi lha myu gu yid du 'ong ba'i 'od kyi dam par thar pa ni/mos pa 
52a1(phal chen): @#//sna tshogs kyi myu gu rnam par 'phel ba dang /yongs su dag par byed pa zhes bya'o//nags tshal gyi lha lo ma dri ma med pa'i phreng ba'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/yon tan gyi dkyil 'khor 
52a2(phal chen): thams cad kyi rgyan yongs su sbyong zhing /rnam par bsgom pa zhes bya'o//nags tshalagyi lha yan lag rab tu 'chad pa'i gzi brjid kyi rnam par thar pa ni/shes rab dri ma med pa'i sgo nas chos kyi dbyings la dmigs 
52a3(phal chen): shing /kun nas rnam par dmigs pas rgyas par 'gengs pa zhes bya'o//nags tshal:gyi lha dge ba'i 'od sna tshogs kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi spyod pa rgya mtsho la yang dag par 'jug pa'i chos kyi 
52a4(phal chen): sprin mngon par sgrub pa zhes bya'o//nags tshalgyi lha sgra skad yid du 'ong ba'i dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa ni sems can thams cad kyi yid du mi 'ong ba'i sgra la bzod pa rab tu thob pas chos kyi snang ba dri ma med pa 
52a5(phal chen): zhes bya'o//nags tshal gyilha kun nas spos mang po ldang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/spyod pa rgya chen po 'di dkyil 'khor spyod pas yongs su dag par byed cing phyogs kun tu rnam par snang ba zhes bya'o//nags 
52a6(phal chen): tshal gyi lha kun nas dpal gyi 'od rab tu 'byung ba'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba la phan pa'i don chos thams cad kyi yon tan gyis rgyas par yang dag par spyod pa zhes bya'o//nags tshalagyi lha 
52a7(phal chen): me tog dang 'bras bu dang ro'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/sangs rgyas skye ba thams cad la dga' bar lta zhing rjes su dran pa'i yon tan gyi rgyan gyi snying po zhes bya'o//de nas nags tshal gyi lha yal ga sna tshogs 
52a8(phal chen): kyi sprin gyi sangs rgyas kyi mthus/nags tshalagyi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bsod nams stobs dang ye shes stobs rnams 
52b1(phal chen): dang //sngon tshe byang chub spyod pas sangs rgyas byung //stobs rnams kun gyi rang byung pha rol phyin//'gro ba kun na 'phags shing rnam par mdzes//'gro ba thams cad snyed kyi thugs rje'i sgo//de bzhin gshegs pas 
52b2(phal chen): sngon tshe rab tu sbyangs//rang byung de bas 'gro ba'i don mdzad do//sna tshogs spungs 'od rnam thar 'di la 'jug/sems can kun gyis bde gshegs mthong ma thag/mos pa rgya mtsho rnam par dag par 'gyur//rgyal ba 
52b3(phal chen): kun gyi lam yang yang dag ston//myu gu yid 'ong 'od kyi rnam thar yin//rgyal ba kun gyis ba spu'i yon tan 'khor//bskal pa rgya mtsho zad par mi nus te//'gro ba kun gyis sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//rnam thar 
52b4(phal chen): 'di ni phreng ba'i dpal gyis mthong //sngan cad rgyal bas sangs rgyas gang gis rnams//zhing mang rdul snyed mchod pa de dag dran//kun tu shes rab dkyil 'khor rnam par sbyangs//yal ga 'od kyis brjid kyis 'di 
52b5(phal chen): mthong ngo //'gro ba kun gyi spyod pa rgya mtsho dag/skad cig gcig gis sangs rgyas thugs su chud//rgyal ba'i ye shes chags med shin tu yangs//sna tshogs 'gyur spungs 'od kyi 'di mthong ngo //bde gshegs 
52b6(phal chen): gsungs ni zhi ba'i rab zhir gsung //dga' ba yangs pa chen po rnam par skyed//sems can mos pa ji bzhin de dag shes//'di ni sgra rnams sgrogs pa'i dbyangs kyi thos//phyogs bcu'i zhing gi dbyings rnams 
52b7(phal chen): rgyas byas shing //sangs rgyas byin brlabs rgya chen rab tu 'byung //rgyal ba'i sngon gyi spyod pa dag kyang ston//kun tu dri zhim 'od dpal 'di la 'jug/ma rung sems can yon tan med pa'i rnams//myos shing bde 
52b8(phal chen): ba kun tu 'khor ba dag/de dag la ni thams cad mkhyen la ma ston//'phags pa'i 'od kyis 'khor gyis 'di mthong ngo //sems can ma rungs sbyor ba spyod pa rnams//bskal pa bye bas sangs rgyas mthong bar 'gyur//
53a1(phal chen): @#//gzhan ni bde gshegs skad cig gcig gis lta//ri'i 'od kyi lha yis 'di mthong ngo//de la ri'i lha rin po che'i rtse mo'i me tog shin tu rgyas par gnas pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/bsam gtan zhi zhing phangs 
53a2(phal chen): la dben pa'i snang ba la rab tu 'jug pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha nags tshal sna tshogs me tog shin tu rgyas pa'i gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba yongs su smin par byed pa'i 
53a3(phal chen): byams pa sgom pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//ri'i lha kun nas shin tu 'phags pas rnam par snang ba'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa rnam par lta ba'i dbang po la 'jug pas 
53a4(phal chen): mngon par snang ba zhes bya'o//ri'i lha rdul thams cad dang mi gnas pas dri ma med pa'i gtsug phud kyi rnam bar thar pa ni/bskal pa rgya mtsho mtha' yas pas mi skyo ba'i brtson 'grus kyis rab tu sbyor ba zhes 
53a5(phal chen): bya'o//ri'i lha snang bas phyogs su gsol ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan mtha' yas pa'i snang ba rnam par bstan pa ston pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha stobs la thub pa med pa'i snang ba'i 
53a6(phal chen): 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir gti mug yongs su sel ba'i 'od ces bya'o//ri'i lha kun gzi brjid dpal gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/chags pa thams 
53a7(phal chen): cad dang mnyam pa'i rgyan gyi sdugs bsngal yang dag par 'joms pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lha snang ba'i rdul chu bo'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thor pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi bstan pa'i yon 
53a8(phal chen): tan snang ba yang dag par ston pa zhes bya'o//ri'i lta kun tu mngon par lta ba'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi rmi lam gyi mthar yang dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par 'phel bar byed pa 
53b1(phal chen): zhes bya'o//ri'i lha kun nas sra ba'i rdo rje'i mtshams kyi mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/don rgya mtsho rgya chen po mtha' yas pa dang /'grogs pa zhes bya'o//de nas ri'i lha rin po che'i rtse mo me tog shin tu rgyas par gnas 
53b2(phal chen): pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/ri'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rgyal ba'i rnam 'phrul mi zad tshad med ltos//sngon tshe 
53b3(phal chen): skyod rab mtha' yas rgyal bas spyod//rnam thar zhing gi rdul snyed bsgom mdzad pas//'gro ba ma lus dga' ba'i shugs kyang skyed//sangs rgyas spu nas dri ma med dpal bkye//yan lag mtshan brgyan 'gro ba kun bka' 
53b4(phal chen): zhing //byams tshul mtha' yas 'jig rten kun la ston//nags tshal rgyas pa'i gtsug gi rnam thar to//sangs rgyas sku ni mtha' yas 'gro bar mngon//'jig rten kun tu phyogs bcu bkang zhing 'phags//
53b5(phal chen): dbang po dang ba'i sems can rangs shing dga'//rnam thar 'di la 'phags pa'i rgyal mtshan 'jug/rang byung mkha' mnyam 'jig rten kun ma chags//bskal pa kun spyod brtson 'grus stobs mi skyo//sna 
53b6(phal chen): tshogs mkhas 'gro sbyong ba ma lus 'dul//dga' ba dri med gtsug phud rnam thar to//'jig rten kun ni mdongs gyur mun stug zhugs//thugs rjes der bskyed sangs rgyas 'od bkye zhing //'gro khams yun 
53b7(phal chen): rings nyal ba sad par mdzad//rigs nas dga' 'od lha'i rnam thar to//srid pa'i rgya mtshor spyad pa sngon spyod tshe//zhing rdul mnyam pa'i sems can gtso mchod pas//de dag dgyes bzhin sems can byang chub 
53b8(phal chen): bkod//zhi ba'i sa 'di stobs 'chad lha 'jug go/las kyi sprin sgrib g.yogs shing bsgribs pa rnams//srid par sems can sdug bsngal 'khor gzigs nas//de dag mun pa ye shes 'od kyis 
54a1(phal chen): @#//bsal//gzi brjid dpal 'od rnam thar 'di sems so//rgyal ba'i spu mas sangs rgyas yon tan kun//phyogs dbyings sems can bsam pa bzhin du grags//rdzu 'phrul rnam pa 
54a2(phal chen): bskal pa kun tu gnas//'od ma rdul gyi chu bo'i lha 'jug go/sems can kun spyod dge ba rgya mtsho kun//de la phyogs bkang sangs rgyas mngon par snang //sna tshogs thabs kyis 'jig rten chos kyang 
54a3(phal chen): ston//tshul mngon mig gi lha yis de thos so//chos sgo rgya mtsho mi zad tshad med pa//sangs rgyas gsung gcig sems can kun la gsung //bskal pa kun brjod 'jig rten mngal ston//rdo rje tshul 
54a4(phal chen): mig 'di yi gnas la 'jug/de la sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun nas shin tu rgyas par dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/skad cig skad cig cing sems can thams cad la byams pa dang /snying rje'i dkyil 
54a5(phal chen): 'khor gyis rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha bsod nams brten pa shin tu bsags pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi stobs dang shugs kyi rgyan ces bya'o//
54a6(phal chen): sa'i lha sems can rin po ches rgyas pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/ {}ting nge 'dzin sna tshogs bsgoms pa rnam par 'byed pa dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha dus su dge ba'i mig rab tu ston pa'i rnam par thar 
54a7(phal chen): pa ni/sems can thams can dga' zhing bde ba dang rtse ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha gser gyi mig gi dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/lus thams cad yongs su dag par byed pa'i tshul ston pa zhes 
54a8(phal chen): bya'o//sa'i lha spu las spos kyi 'od rnam par 'gyed pa'i rnam par thar pa ni /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yon tan rgya mtsho thams cad rnam par sgom pa brjod pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha sgra dbyangs yid du 
54b1(phal chen): 'ong ba bsgrogs pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad smra ba'i sgra rgya mtsho byin gyis rlob pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha me tog gi klong sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing phun sum tshogs pa'i rang 
54b2(phal chen): bzhin dri ma med pa zhes bya'o//sa'i lha kun nas rdo rje'i rdul gyis rgyud kyi rnam par thar pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams cad kyi 'khor lo byin gyi rlabs kun nas rgyu zhing 'byung ba zhes bya'o//sa'i lha khyim dri ma med pa'i 
54b3(phal chen): 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad kyi tshul la yang dag par 'jug pa'i chos kyi rgyan bsgom pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas sa'i lha dpal gyi me tog kun nas shin tu rgyas pa dri ma med pas/sangs 
54b4(phal chen): rgyas kyi mthus sa'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sngon cad skad cig skad cig bde gshegs kyis//brjod du med pa'i snying 
54b5(phal chen): rje'i sgo rnams dang //byams pa'i sgo yang mi zad sgoms pas na//de tshe sangs rgyas sku ni mi shigs sra//dus gsum gtogs pa'i sems can bsod nams dang //snying rje byang chub sems dpa'i bsod nams 
54b6(phal chen): dag/rgyal ba'i spu gcig de snyed bsod nams 'od//bsod nams de mthong rab tu dga' bar 'gyur//'byung ba med cing byon pa gang yang med//ting 'dzin zhi ba rgya mtsho rab tu zhugs//sems can rgyan gyis 
54b7(phal chen): 'di rab tu ston//'di ni sna tshogs shing gi ze ba'i sa yin no//sngon cad thub pas spyod pa spyad pa dang //'gro ba'i sgrib pa rnam par zlog pa ni//rin chen bkram pa'i 'od kyi sa yin te//rnam thar de la dga' 
54b8(phal chen): bar lha lta'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa ni mtha' yas te//'jig rten kun tu skad cig cig la ston//dus tshod yang dag ston pa'i sa yi lha//dga' bar sangs rgyas yul la rnam par lta//sangs rgyas 
55a1(phal chen): @#//gsung ni mtha' yas bsam mi khyab//sems can kun bsgrags nyon mongs zhi bar mdzad//gser 'od mig gi dpal ni rab dga' ba//sangs rgyas 'od tan mtha' yas mnga' ba ste//sangs 
55a2(phal chen): rgyas sprul pas rnam par kun gzugs su//phyogs kun chos kyi dbyings su rgyas par mdzad//sprul pa dri zhim 'od kyis yi lha//sangs rgyas sems can 'dul ba de la lta//phyogs rnams yid 'od dbyangs kyis 
55a3(phal chen): gang byas shing //'gro ba 'di ni phyi mtha'i bskal par brjod//yid du 'ong ba'i sgra dbyangs sa'i lha//rab tu dga' bar sangs rgyas dbyangs la nyan//rgyal ba'i spu khas sems can mnyam pa yi//spos kyi 'od 
55a4(phal chen): bkye 'jig rten kun bkang zhing //sems can ma lus yongs su smin mdzad cing //me tog gtsug phud 'byang bas yang dag mthong //de bzhin gshegs sku rdo rje lta bu ste//mtshams su mi phyed sra ba'i ri bo 
55a5(phal chen): 'dra//'gro ba kun gyis sangs rgyas bskyed mi nus//rdo rje rdul gyi lha ni des dga' 'o//de la grong khyer gyi lha rin chen rtse mo'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni//sems can la phan pa yang dag par sbyar ba 'byung ba 
55a6(phal chen): zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha rgyan rnam pa sna tshogs 'byung ba'i rnam par thar pa ni//'gro ba'i dbang po yongs su smin par byed pa'i 'dul ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha'i rin chen dri med shin tu dag pa'i 
55a7(phal chen): rnam par thar pa ni//sem can thams cad bsod nams dang dga' ba myong bar byed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lta smos pa'i mya ngan thams cad spong bas rnam par snang ba'i rnam par thar pa na/skyob pa dang mi 
55a8(phal chen): 'jigs pa dang /snying rje chen po zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lga sgron ma shin tu rgyas pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/shes rab kyi kun tu rnam par 'byed pa zhes bya'o//grong khyed kyi lha 'od 'phro ba'i dpal 
55b1(phal chen): mngon par 'bar ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/thabs kyi sgo kun nas ston pa'i mthu zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyis lha bsod nams kyi shugs snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'gro ba kun gyis bsod nams 
55b2(phal chen): 'byung ba'i snang ba zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha lus dri ma med pas mnyam pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/sems can rmongs pa thams cad la rmi lam rnam par ston pa'i 'od ces bya'o//grong khyer gyi lha spos kyi rgyal 
55b3(phal chen): mtshan gtsug phud kyi rgyan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/nyon mongs pa dang mi dge ba'i dri ma rnam par sel cing thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi dri yang dag par bskyad pa zhes bya'o//grong khyer gyi lta rin chen dpal gyi 'od kyi rnam 
55b4(phal chen): par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad kyi sgrib pa'i ri rnam par 'joms pa'i 'od ces bya'o//de nas grong khyer gyi lha rin po che'i rtse mo'i 'od kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthus/grong khyer gyi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa 
55b5(phal chen): thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//mgon po'i chos nyid bsam gyis mi khyab ste//snang ba brnyes shing phyogs su rnam par mdzes//sems can mtha' yas yongs 
55b6(phal chen): su smin mdzad pa'i//sangs rgyas 'gro ba kun la mngon du snang //sems can thams cad dbang po sna tshogs te//de dag ma lus rgyal bas 'gro bar mkhyen//rnam thar 'di ni rab tu thob pa'i phyir//sna tshogs 
55b7(phal chen): 'od 'byung lha yang rab tu dga'//rgyal bas bskal ba mtha' yas yang dag spyad//sngon chad rgyal bas chos kyang yang dag bzung //dga' ba'i yid kyis rtag tu de dag blangs//rnam thar 'di ni dga' ba'i 
55b8(phal chen): 'od kyis mthong //'gro ba 'dir ni thugs rje byams bskyed cing //de bzhin gshegs pas sngon chad sems can rnams//kun gyi kyang nam mtha' yas rnam par zlog/nyon mongs yongs su sbyong ba 'di la 
56a1(phal chen): @#//dga'//rgyal bas shes rab rgya che mtha' yas te//tshad med nam mkha' 'dra zhing mtshungs pa yin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i shes rab rnam spyod pas//mar me'i me tog 
56a2(phal chen): 'od kyi lha dga' 'o//de bzhin gshegs pas 'gro mnyam sku gzugs rnams//'gro ba kun gyi bsam pa rnams la bstan//de 'dra'i tshul la rab tu bsgoms pa yis//mngon 'od 'phro ba'i lha de rab tu 
56a3(phal chen): dga'//bsod nams rgya mtsho rgya chen mtha' yas pa//sngon chad de bzhin gshegs pas yongs su sbyangs//de 'dra'i rnam par thar yas rnam lta bas//bsod nams dpal gyi 'od de rab tu dga'//
56a4(phal chen): srid par rab tu rmongs pa'i sems can rnams//'khor gyur rab rib mdongs pa gzigs pas na//de dag phan phyir sangs rgyas mngon du byung //dri med 'od kyis rnam thar 'di thob bo//de bzhin gshegs pa'i
56a5(phal chen): rdzu 'phrul stobs la ltos//'gro la sprin 'bras mtha' dang dbus yang med//de dag bstan cing 'gro ba rnam par 'dul//spos kyi rgyal mtshan 'od kyis 'di mthong ngo //sems can rmongs pa mun pas mdongs 
56a6(phal chen): gyur cing //rnam pa sna tshogs sgrib pas bsgribs gyur pa//sangs rgyas 'od kyis rnam par kun shes 'gyur//rtse mo'i 'od kyi lha ni 'di la 'jug/de la snying po byang chub kyi lha dge ba 'dri ma med pa'i rgyun 
56a7(phal chen): gyis brgyan pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas mang po mchod pa yang dag par 'gyur zhing /sngon gyi smon lam gyi rgyan ston pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha ri rab spo'i rin po che'i 
56a8(phal chen): chos kyi dam par thar pa ni/'gro ba thams cad na mngon sum du byang chub kyi spyod pa yang dag par bsgrub cing 'byung ba zhes bya'o//snying po byang cub kyi lha dbyangs kyi 'brug sgra sgrogs pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam bar 
56b1(phal chen): thar pa ni/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa la sangs rgyas ston cing rmi lam gyi mthar/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 'brug sgra sgrogs pa zhes bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha me tog gi char sna tshogs 'bab 
56b2(phal chen): pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/dga' ba thams cad yongs su gtong zhing rin po che'i char 'bab pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//snying po byang chubakyi lha 'od 'phro mang po shin tu dri ma med pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/'dul ba'i 
56b3(phal chen): sems can yongs su smin par byed cing snying po byang chub kyi phun sum tshogs pa sna tshogs ston pa zhes bya'o//snying po byed chub kyi lha me tog gi phreng ba 'od kyi gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can tham
56b4(phal chen): sa cad kyi dbang po bzhin du chos kyi tshul zab mo dran pa ston pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//snying po byang cub kyi lha rin po che'i rgyan gyi char rab tu 'bebs pa'i rnam par thar pa ni dga' ba yang dag par myong ba mtha' yas 
56b5(phal chen): pa la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//snying po bya chub kyi lha mthu dam pa spos kyi mig gi rnam par thar ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang legs pa rab tu brjod pa'i byin gyis rlabs zhes bya'o//snying po byang
56b6(phal chen): chub kyi lha rdo rje sna tshogs kyi sprin gyi rnam par thar pa ni/snying po byang chub rnam pa mtha' yas pa yang dag par ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//snying po byang chub kyi lha pad+mo'i shing snang ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni byang 
56b7(phal chen): chub kyi shing yongs su zhi bas phyogs rgyas par byed pa zhes bya'o//snying po byed chub kyi lha dge ba'i lta ba sna tshogs rnam par ston pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs sna tshogs yang 
56b8(phal chen): dag par rgyu ba zhes bya'o//de nas snying po byang chub kyi lha dge ba dri ma med pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi sangs rgyas kyi mthus/snying po byang chub kyi lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa 
57a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//bskal pa mang po mtha' dang dbus med par//de bzhin gshegs pas ji ltar sngon spyad dran//
57a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas thams cad mchod cing bsgrubs pas na//de phyir de yi bsod nams nam mkha'i tshad//zhing rnams mtha' yas tshad med 'dra med par//mi zad gtong ba'i sgo nas sngon btang bas//ri bo spo 
57a3(phal chen): ye 'od kyi lha de ni//bde gshegs dran zhing rab tu dga' bar 'gyur//bde gshegs sprul pa rnam pa mtha' yas pa//de dag zhing rnams kun tu rab tu rgyu//'gro bar sangs rgyas rmi lam bzhin ston 
57a4(phal chen): cing //sprin gyi sgra dbyangs lha de rab tu dga'//mtha' yas bskal par dka' ba rab spyad do//dang ba'i spyan gyis 'gro la phan pa'i phyir//rgya mtsho'i spyan yang yongs su sngon btang ste//de la me tog mig 
57a5(phal chen): gi lha dga' 'o//yon tan rin chen 'od 'phro mtha' yas sprin//'gro ba kun tu snying po byang chub nas//rab tu bkye ba'i sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul gyis//'od 'phro 'gyed pa'i lus kyi lha yang dga'//sems 
57a6(phal chen): can rnams kyi spyod pa rgya mtsho ste//de las rgyal ba chos kyi char yang 'bebs//ji ltar mos par nem nur rnam par sel//de yis gtsug phud 'phyang ba dga' ba thob//rgyan gyi cher 'bebs mig 
57a7(phal chen): bzang lha de ni//sems kyi skad cig gcig gis tshad med pa'o//chos don dbyings kyi bye brag mtha' yas pa//rgya mtsho tha dad yang dag shes pas 'jug/zhing rnams brjod du med pa thams cad du//
57a8(phal chen): 'gro ba kun la sangs rgyas legs brjod pas//de phyir yon tan sangs rgyas zhing kun grags//de ni rjes su spos mig lta yang dran//mtha' yas shing gi rnam pa sna tshogs gzugs//
57b1(phal chen): byang chub shing la kun kyang ma lus bstan//rdo rje sna tshogs sprin gyi lha de ni//byang chub shing la dga' bar rnam par lta//sangs rgyas ye shes snying po byang chub la//phyogs rnams kun tu mtha' dang mu med de//
57b2(phal chen): pad+mo snang zhing gsal ba'i lha de ni//rnam thar rnam yangs 'di la rab dga' 'o//sangs rgyas stobs rnams yongs dag bsam mi khyab//snying po byang chub 'dir ni kun kyang grag/yang dag bsgrubs pas byung ba de 
57b3(phal chen): dag ni//thams cad sna tshogs snang ba'i 'od nyan to//de la rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi phyag rgya'i rnam par thar pa ni/dga' ba yangs pa rin po che'i char gyis kun tu khyab pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 
57b4(phal chen): 'gro pad+mo snang ba'i 'od zer gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi sku 'od zer gyi kha dog pad+mo'i dra ba thams cad du ston pas dga' bar byed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gi 'gro ma tog gtsug phud kyi dpal dri ma med pa'i 
57b5(phal chen): rnam par thar pa ni/sems kyi skad cig thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi dbyibs dang rgyan 'byung ba zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro shin tu mdzes par gnon pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/gom 
57b6(phal chen): pa 'dor ba thams cad mtha' yas pa 'dul ba'i rgyan ces bya'o//rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpa' gyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/skad cig gcig gi pad+mo'i dra ba sna tshogs sprul pa byed cing rin po che'i char pa dang 
57b7(phal chen): dbyangs dang snang ba kun tu 'gyed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'go dga' ba'i dbyangs snyan pa'i rnam par thar pa ni dga' ba shugs dang nyams bde ba'i rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//rkangagis 'gro shing tsan dan 
57b8(phal chen): gyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad du spros kyi ngad sprin du ldang ba zhes bya'o//rkang gi 'gro bad+mo yid du 'ong ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/pad+mo'i gsab thams cad nas yid du 'ong ba'i chos 
58a1(phal chen): @#//kyi dbyings 'byung bas snang ba skyed pa zhes bya'o//rkang gis 'gro rdul gyi shugs kyi 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/lus kyi 'od zer rnam pa sna tshogs thams cad kyi dra bas snang ba byed pa zhes 
58a2(phal chen): bya'o//rkang gis 'gro me tog mang po'i dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad la dge ba'i rtsa ba rgya mtshos yang dag par skul ba 'byung ba zhes bya'o//de nas rkang gis 'gro rin chen dpal gyi 
58a3(phal chen): phyag rgya sangs rgyas kyi mthus/rkang gis 'gro ba'i lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//dga' ba rgya mtsho'i tshul gyis shugs bskyed 
58a4(phal chen): cing //bskal pa mtha' yas sdod chad spyod pa mdzad//zhing rnams kun tu rdul shin tu phra mnyam pa'i//sangs rgyas rnams kyang dgyes pa'i thugs kyi mchod//skad cig sems la pad mo brjod yangs pa//dri zhim 
58a5(phal chen): sna tshogs mngon par sprul mdzad nas//zhing rnams kun tu rgyal ba gshegs mdzad pa//snang ba'i 'od zer ltar des rab tu mthong //'dren pa rnams kyi chos nyid bsam mi khyab//'khor rnams kun tu 'byam klas 
58a6(phal chen): mtha' yas pa'i//rnam par 'phrul pa mi zad rab tu ston//me tog gtsug phud lha des de mthong ngo //phyogs bcu'i zhing gi dbyings rnams ma lus pa//thams cad kun tu gshegs shing bzhud pa yi//
58a7(phal chen): sems can mtha' yas yongs su smin par mdzad//shin tu mdzes pas dga' bar de mthong ngo //sems can snyed kyi sprul pa kun 'gros pas//re re'i sku yi chos kyi dbyings kyang 'gengs//rin chen dri med 'od 
58a8(phal chen): kyi rab tu gtong //rkang bgrod las kyi mtshan gyi 'di mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul mtha' yas te//'khor du rgyal ba chos kyi char yang 'bebs//bde gshegs de'i 'khor na dgar gnas 
58b1(phal chen): shing //dga' sgra dbyangs kyi rnam thar de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas dbyangs ni mkha' dang mnyam pa ste//dbyangs kyi yan lag yon tan kun dang ldan//'jig rten kun dga' sems can rnam par 'du la//tsan dan shing 
58b2(phal chen): gi 'od ni de la nyan//spu rtse kun nas rgyal ba rnam 'phrul dang //dus gsum gshegs pa'i rgyal ba'i mtshan rnams kyi//sgra bsgrags thos shing dga' ba skyer 'gyur te//de ni rdo rje 'od kyi lhas mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs 
58b3(phal chen): sku bsam gyis mi khyab ste//gom pa gom par yon tan rgya mtsho ston//de dag sems can ji ltar mos pas mthong //rdul gyi 'od kyi lhas ni de mthong ngo //'jig rten mgon po rnam 'phrul rnam mang gis//phyogs 
58b4(phal chen): bcur sems can yang dag skul ba mdzad//me tog 'khor mang rkang pas 'gro ba'i lha//mthong zhing dga' ba yangs pa rab tu 'thob//de la lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha khrel dang dag pas rgyas pa'i 'od kyi 
58b5(phal chen): rnam par thar pa ni//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sngon gyi smon lam rjes su dran pas rnam par dpyod pa zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'ilha 'od zer phyogs su snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams mu 
58b6(phal chen): med par sangs rgyas kyi 'od rnam par ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//lus kyi ris su rtogs pa'i lha sgras 'dul ba rgya mtsho'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi gsung 'dul ba'i sems can thams cad la brjod cing yang 
58b7(phal chen): dag par bskul bas rgal 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris gtogs pa'i lha rgyan bzang po'i me tog gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku nam mkha'i mtshan nyid kyi byin gyis rlabs kun 
58b8(phal chen): nas rab tu gnas pa 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lusakyi ris su gtogs pa'i lus spyod yul mtha' yas pa lam gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi yul 'gro ba rnams la yang dag par ston pa'i mthu zhes bya'o//
59a1(phal chen): @#//lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha snang ba'i phreng ba 'dzin pa'i dpal gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can thams cad gsal ba dang 'dras pa'i mdangs kyis dga' ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris 
59a2(phal chen): su gtogs pa'i lha spos dri ma med pa'i sprin las char 'bebs pa'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sem can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i dri ma mngon par sel ba'i sbyor ba zhes bya'o//lus kyi ri su gtogs 
59a3(phal chen): pa'i lha srung 'dzin yongs su sdug pa'i rnam par thar pa ni/semams can thams cad kyi bdud kyi las dang rmongs pa rnam par zlog pa zhes bya'o//lus kyi ris su gtog pa'i lha sgo kun nas yongs su gnon pa la 
59a4(phal chen): mngon du gyur pa'i mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi gnas su rgyal ba rnam par dmigs par mngon du ston pa'i rgyan ces bya'o//lus kyi ris su btogs pa'i lha khrel dang dpal 
59a5(phal chen): mi g.yo ba'i 'od kyi rnam par thar ba ni/sems can thams can kyi dge ba'i shugs thams cad yang dag par 'dzin cing /yang dag par sbyor ba zhes bya'o//de nas lus kyi ris su gtogs pa'i lha khrel dang 
59a6(phal chen): dge bas rgyas pa'i 'od kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthus/lus kyi ris su gtogs pa lha'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ri rab rdul 
59a7(phal chen): phran shin tu phra snyed kyi//bskal pa de dag pha snga'i bskal pa ni//rdul ces bya bar 'od mdzes 'od zer rgyal//mchod de smon lam btab pa bdag gis dran//sangs rgyas sku las 'od 'phro rab bkye ba//de'i 'od 
59a8(phal chen): zer chos kyi dbyings su rgyu//rgyal ba'i 'od zer dra ba sems can 'dul//'od 'phro 'od zer lha yis de mthong ngo //dbyangs kyi yan lag yon tan kun rgyas pa'i//sangs rgyas gsung ni zhing rnams kun tu sgrogs//
59b1(phal chen): 'gro ba ma lus thams cad yang dag bskul//dbyangs kyis 'dul ba de la dga' bas nyan//rgyal ba'i sku ni rab tu zhib ltos//sku gzugs du ma'ang mtshan nyid mi mnga' ste//'gro ba kun na'ang bzhugs par rab tu snang //me tog 
59b2(phal chen): gtsug gis ji bzhin nyid du mthong //'dren pa'i chos nyid bsam gyis mi khyab ste//sems can de dag dad pa ci bzhin du//la la bzhugs dang bzhengs dang gshegs par mthong //de yang spyod lam 'byung ba'i lha mthong ngo //
59b3(phal chen): rgyal ba'i tshems kyis mtshams rnams thams cad nas//dri zhim 'od 'phro sgron ma'i sprin bkye bas//'gro ba kun gyi nyon mongs zhi bar 'gyur//dri med spos kyi sprin gyis de mthong ngo //sangs rgyas mgon po 'gro ba'i skyabs 
59b4(phal chen): su byung //nyon mongs shin tu sdug bsngal gzir ba rnams//mgu bar mdzad cing nyon mongs thams cad bsal//snang ba'i phreng ba 'char bas 'di mthong ngo //'dren pa bskal pa brgyar yang shin tu dkon//rab byung 'jig 
59b5(phal chen): rten don mdzad skyes pa ni//'jig rten kun gyi dbul ba rnam par sel//khrel yod dpal gyi 'od snang de skad smra//sems can nyon mongs sgrib pas nyon mongs shing //bdud kyi las kyis dkrugs te 'phyin pa'i rnams//
59b6(phal chen): de dag rgyal ba rnam thar lam ston to//srung zhing skyong ba'i dpal gyis de mthong ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa ltos//sangs rgyas sprin gyis chos kyi dbyings man chad cing //'jig rten dbang po gnas 
59b7(phal chen): su sems can 'dul//kun sgo'i mig gi dpal gyi spyod yul lo//de la lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/yon tan mtha' yas pa dang /gzugs kyi sku rnam par dpyod pa yang dag par 
59b8(phal chen): ston pa zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje nyi ma'i shugs kyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi ba spu las nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi sprin sna tshogs kyi rgyan 'byung ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rji ri rab me tog 
60a1(phal chen): @#//'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/rnam par 'phrul pa rgya chen po bstan pas/yon tan mtha' yas pa'i lus su 'phrul pa zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje rnam dag sprin gyi dbyangs kyi rnam par thar pa 
60a2(phal chen): ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs mtha' yas tshad med pa'i rjes su song ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rja 'dab po shin tu mang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/'jig rten gyi dbang po thams cad kyi rnam par 'phrul pa ston cing /
60a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis yang dag par skul ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdorje yid du 'ong ba'i rnam par thar pa ni sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi dbyings rab tu bye ba thams cad ma lus pa la yang dag par spyod pa zhes bya'o//
60a4(phal chen): lag na rdo rje shing gi sgra dbyangs kyi rnam par thar ba ni/shing gi lha thams cad 'dren pa'i rgyan yid du 'ong bas brgyan pa zhes bya'o//lag na rdor rje seng ge'i 'od kyi rgyal po'i rnam par thar pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsod nams 
60a5(phal chen): kyi dkyil 'khor yangs pa'i tshogs kyi rjes su 'brang ba'i yod ces bya'o//lag na rdo rje 'od 'phro stug po'i dpal gyi mig gi rnam par thar pa ni/sems can ma rungs pa'i rgyud la lus 'jigs 'jigs ltar rab tu ston cing kun tu 
60a6(phal chen): rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//lag na rdo rje rin chen gtsug phud pad mo'i 'od kyi rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa'i rgyan thams cad kyis brgyan pa'i gtsug phud gi nor bu rin po che thams cad kyi char mngon par 'bebs 
60a7(phal chen): pa zhes bya'o//de nas lag na rdo rje mthu chen kha dog bzang po'i 'od kyi sangs rgyas kyi mthus/lag na rdo rje'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//
60a8(phal chen): chos rgyal chos kyis mdzes pa yi//chos nyid de la rnam par ltos//yon tan mtha' yas sku gzugs kyi//'jig rten kun tu rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas ba spu thams cad nas//'od zer dra ba bsam mi khyab//
60b1(phal chen): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'dra ba ste//phyogs bcu kun tu rnam par mdzes//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul de dag gis//chos kyi dbyings su khyab byas shing //'gro ba kun la mngon sum du//mi zad sku ni shin tu ston//sangs 
60b2(phal chen): rgyas gsung gi dkyil 'khor ni//zhing rnams kun tu rab tu sgrog/gsung gis sems can thams cad kyis//bsam pa thams cad rab tu 'gengs//'jig rten dbang po kun gnas na//thub pa rab tu bzhugs 
60b3(phal chen): par snang //de dag thams cad 'khor rnams la//'dren pa drag gi chos ston mdzad//rgya mtsho chos kyi sgo bskul bas//gzhan dang gzhan gyi tshul dag gis//thams cad chos kyi bye brag don//rgyal ba zad 
60b4(phal chen): med rab tu ston//rgyal chen mtha' med tshul dag la//rgya mtsho 'khor rnams rab tu 'jug/sangs rgyas 'od zer rab 'dres pas//kun gyis 'dren pa mthong bar 'gyur//zhing rnams bye ma'i rdul snyed du//
60b5(phal chen): rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kun mchod pas//bsod nams rgya chen nam mkha' bzhin//kun gyis 'dren pa rab tu mthong //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul ma 'dres pa//thams cad zhing du ston pa mdzad//'gro ba sangs rgyas 
60b6(phal chen): mngon sum du//byang chub snying por rnam par mdzes//'od zer sna tshogs dkyil 'khor gyi//'od 'phro sprin ni kun tu snang //chos dbyings kun tu khyab byas shing //sangs rgyas spyod yul rab tu ston//de la byang chub 
60b7(phal chen): sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i rnam par thar pa ni//tshul rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab pas//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yon tan rgya mtsho la 'jug ste/'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad yongs su dag par bya ba dang 
60b8(phal chen): 'gro ba gdul ba la mthar thug par mngon par sgrub pa zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar pa dag/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin pas/yon tan gyi tshogs thams cad kyi yul zhes bya ba'i rnam par thar/

61b1(phal chen): lus kun nang skye//'on kyang bde gshegs gshegs dang 'thob med//zhing rnams kun tu dul yang rab tu ston//sa yi tshul la 'jug pa tshad med de//spyod pa thub pas byang chub sems dpa' bstan//bsam gyis mi

62a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad du mngon par byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du 'gro ba'i rgyan byed pa zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' yon tan 'od mchog kun tu sgron ma'i rnam par 
62a2(phal chen): thar pa ni/sems skad cig gis mngon par rdzogs par byang chub pa'i sgo zad ma shes pa ston cing /chos ston gyi dbyings bsam gyis mi khyab pas yongs su smin pa dang rnam par 'dul bar byed pa'i 'od ces 
62a3(phal chen): bya'o//byang chub sems dpa'i kun tu 'bar ba seng ge'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs dang zhing yang dag par 'byung ba la rnam par lta ba zhes

62b5(phal chen): snying po byang chub tu//byang chub 'tshang rgya rnam par 'phrul pa mdzad//bskal pa brgya phrag stong rnams tshad med du//de bzhin gshegs pas sngan chad spyod spyad de//nam mkha' 'dra bar byas nas rab bkye zhing //
62b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas zhing 'di sngon tshe rab tu sbyangs//mtha' dang dbus med bskal par la la yi//skyo ba med par rgyal ba'i yul bsams kyang //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul stobs la dbus med pa//phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal
