62b8(phal chen): snang //bskal pa brgya stong tshad med pa dag tu//rgyal ba'i ba spu gcig mtha' shes par dag pa//tshul rnams kun sgor sangs rgyas chags pa med//'gro ba 'di ni rgya chen bsam yas mdzes//
63a1(phal chen): @#//sngon tshe'i bskal par rgyal bas 'gro kun tu//sangs rgyas rgya mtsho rgya chen bsnyen bkur bas//de bas 'jig rten gtso ma chos dbyings rnams//kun nas mchod pa mang po rgya chen 'byung //phyogs rnams 
63a2(phal chen): bkang ste sangs rgyas 'byung ba dag/rdul gyi sbubs su tshad med zhing yang ston//de dag yul du mi zad mtha' yas pa'i//bskal par rgya mtshor byin gyis rlob pa mdzad//sngon tshe'i bskal par rgyas pas 'gro don spyad//thugs 
63a3(phal chen): rje'i rgya mtsho tshad med rab skyed cing //'gro ba rgya mtsho kun nas 'khor ba yi//'khor rnams rgya mtsho mtha' yas kun kyang sbyangs//sangs rgyas mtshan med sku med rdul dang bral//de bzhin nyid gnas chos dbyings snying po ste//'gro 
63a4(phal chen): ba kun na mtha' yas gzugs su snang //'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal kun zhi mdzad//de la byang chub sems dpa' 'od zer rgya mtsho 'od ces dpal gyi zla ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/pha rol tu phyin pa dang sa'i shugs kyi sems can 
63a5(phal chen): kyi khams kyi tshul dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa yang dag par 'byung ba zhes bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' sprin dpa' bo'i rgya mtsho dri med 'od kyi snying po'i rnam par thar pa ni/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi sems 
63a6(phal chen): skad cig la chos kyi dbyings kyi tshul gyis khyim sna tshogs la rnam par lta ba zhes bya'o//byang chub kyi sems dpa' yon tan rin chen 'byung ba ye shes dpal gyi gtsug phud kyi rnam par thar pa ni/bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par 
63a7(phal chen): 'gro ba rnams la mngon sum du yon tan dri ma med pas yongs su dag pa sgrub pa zhes bya'o//byang chub sem dpa' rgya chen 'od 'bar blo gros dbang phyug gi rnam par thar pa ni/byangs chub sems dpa' thams can kyi snying po byang 
63a8(phal chen): chub tu thog ma 'gro ba'i ye shes kyi phyogs yang dag par 'byung ba'i rgyun ces bya'o//byang chub sems pa'i pad mo rnam par gnod pa 'od zer gtsug phud kyi rnam par thal ba ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad tha mi dad par 'gro 
63b1(phal chen): pa thams cad kyi dbang po dang /mos pa rgya mtsho'i 'od ces bya'o//byang chub sems dpa' kun tu ye shes sprin gyi nyi ma'i rgyal mtshan snang ba'i rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas ye shes bsgrub par bskal pa mtha' yas pa dag tu byin gyis rlob 
63b2(phal chen): cing rnam par spyod pa zhes bya'o//byang chub sems pa'i mthu stobs brtson 'grus rdo rje yangs pa'i snying po'i rnam par thar pa ni/chos thams cad kyi phyag rgya mtha' dang dbus med pa la yang dag par 'jug pa zhes bya'o//byang chub
63b3(phal chen): sems dpa' spos kyi 'od 'phro shin tu 'bar ba'i 'od kyi rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thar pa ni/sang rgyas da ltar byung ba thams cad kyi thog ma'i byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa shes pa'i dkyil 'khor yang dag par bsgrub pa zhes 
63b4(phal chen): bya'o//byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par snang mdzad yon tan yangs pa'i rgyal mtshan gyi rnam par thal ba ni/rnam par snang mdzad kyi smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad kyi dbyings la rab tu gnas pa zhes bya'o//byang chub 
63b5(phal chen): sems dpa' bsod nams yangs pa shin tu 'phags pa'i 'od mchog yon tan 'byung ba'i blo gros kyi rnam par thar pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi yul zab mo chos kyi dbyings thams cad la kun nas rab tu gnas pa rnam par spyod pa zhes 
63b6(phal chen): bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' 'od zer rgya mtsho 'od can dpal gyi zla bas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus byang chub sems dpa' rnam par phye ba rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag 
63b7(phal chen): smras so//pha rol phyin pa rgya mtsho bsam yas dang /sa yi dkyil 'khor rgya chen kun kyang yongs su rgyas//tshad med sems can rnam par 'bul ba'i tshe//sangs rgyas zhing yangs rnams kyang rnam par sbyangs//phyogs 
63b8(phal chen): bcu'i sangs rgyas zhing kun rgyas byas te//sangs rgyas sems can khams sbyin 'dul mdzad pa//skad cig thugs kyi mchos kyi 'khor lo dag/mtha' yas 'gro mnyam bsdam pa tshim par ston//bskal pa rgya 
64a1(phal chen): @#//che mi zad mu med par//rgyal ba 'gro la mngon sum kun nas bzhugs//mgon pos sngon cad ji ltar sbyangs tshul du//rgyal bas spyod yul rnam dag rab tu bstan//chos rgyal de dag ji ltar 
64a2(phal chen): rnam 'phrul ba//phyogs rnams rgya mtsho ma lus kun na snang //snying po byang chub kun na'ang rgyal ba dag/'khor gyis bskor cing chos rnams gsung ba snang //'gro ba'i bsam pa thams cad tshim mdzad cing //
64a3(phal chen): 'jig rten dag na sangs rgyas tshul kun mdzes//dbang po ji bzhin chos kyi char yang 'bebs//sangs rgyas chos sku yangs pa rnam par mdzes//de bzhin nyid ltar mtshan nyid lus med de//rgyal ba dri med 'od 
64a4(phal chen): gsal chos kyi sku//ye shes zhi ba'i sku ni tshad med de//phyogs dbyings kun bkang chos kyi char yang 'beb pa//chos rje thams cad stobs kyis rnam par dag/bde gshegs ye shes mkha' 'dra mtha' 
64a5(phal chen): yas te//rgyal chen 'gro ba mtha' dbus med bkang zhing //rgyal ba stobs la 'jug pa rab tu ston//sangs rgyas 'od kyis chos dbyings kun bkang zhing //sangs rgyas spyod pa thams cad rdzogs par bstan//
64a6(phal chen): rgyal bas sngon tshe ji ltar rnam sbyangs pas//de bzhin ye shes sa rnams rgyal bas bstan//phyogs rnams kun tu rang byung rnam par mdzes//smon lam rnam par 'phrul pa ma lus ston//ji ltar sngon tshe rgyal bas 
64a7(phal chen): yongs sbyangs pa//rgyal ba'i 'od zer dra ba kun nas sgrog/sangs rgyas yul ni phyogs su zad pa med//dbus dang mtha' med gzhal med mtha' yas dbyings//chos dbyings kun tu chags med brnyes pa ni//sangs 
64a8(phal chen): rgyas zhing rnams kun na shin tu mdzes//de'i tshe bcom ldan 'das kyi seng ge'i khri dang //pad+mo'i ze ba'i dri thams cad dang //nor bu'i rgyal po thams cad kyis nges dang //'khor lo'i lte ba thams cad dang //me tog gi ze 
64b1(phal chen): mi dri thams cad dang /stegs bu'i ngos thams cad dang //rta babs kyi sgo thams cad dang /nor bu'i gzugs kyi ngos thams cad dang /rin po che 'od 'phro ba'i sgo thams cad dang /'od zer sgron ma'i sgo thams cad dang /seng+ge'i khri'i rgyan 
64b2(phal chen): gyi sgo thams cad nas//sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra bsnyed kyi byang chub sems dpa' mang po 'thon par 'gyur te/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i blo gros rnam par rol ba'i ye shes mngon par shes pa'i rgyal 
64b3(phal chen): po zhes bya ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' sgra dbyangs kun tu sgrog pa dang /byang chub sems dba' nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gtsug phud dang /byang chub sems dang ye shes chen pos nyi ma yis rab tu 
64b4(phal chen): gnon pa'i blo gros dang /byang chub sems dma' yon tan rin po che bsam gyis mi khyab pa ye shes rgya mtsho'i dpal dang /byang chub sems dad dmigs rgyas padamo'i sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dang /byang chu ba sems dpa' nam 
64b5(phal chen): mkha'i tshul padamo sna tshogs kyi gtsug phud dag/byang chub sems dpa' gser gyi 'od 'phro gzi brjid dkyil 'khor gyi 'od dang /byang chub sems pa'i kun tu sga chos kyi dbyings kyi dbyings dang /byang chub sems 
64b6(phal chen): dpa' sprin yid du 'ong ba dri med pa'i zla ba dang /de dag la stsogs pa byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra bsnyed mchod pa'i sprin rnam pa sna tshogs rab tu 'gyed cing /'di ltar nor bu rin po che'i me tog gi sprin thams cad ni rab tu 
64b7(phal chen): 'byin/spos kyi sprin thams cad ni shin tu 'gyed/rin po che'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi sprin thams cad ni mngon par 'byin/yul mtha' yas pa'i spos kun tu 'phro ba'i bdug pa'i sprin ni/rnam par 'gyed cing nor bu nyi ma'i snying po 
64b8(phal chen): 'khor lo'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyis sprin ni rab tu gtong /sil snyan gyi sgra dbyangs yid du 'od pa'i sprin ni rab tu sgrog/rin po che 'od 'phro ba kha dog mtha' yas pa'i sgron ma'i sprin gyis ni rab tu snang bar byed/nor bu sna tshogs 
65a1(phal chen): @#//kyi shing gi yal ga las me tog gi kha dog ni rnam par spyod/rin po che'i 'od dri ma med pa zad mi shes pa'i nor bu'i rgyal po'i char ni rab tu 'bebs/rgyan mdzes pa thams cad yang dag par ston pa'i 
65a2(phal chen): nor bu'i rgyal po'i sprin gyi char ni rab tu 'bebs shing /de lta bu dag la sogs pa byang chub sems dpa' re re zhing yang sangs rgyas kyi zhing rdul shing tu phra ba snyed kyi mched pa'i sprin gyi char rab tu 'bebs pa byed cing lhags 
65a3(phal chen): par gyur te/bcom ldan 'das la lan brgya stong du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas te/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho yod pa thams cad du mchod pa'i sprin rgyun mi 'chad pa'i char mngon par phab nas gang nas 'ongs 
65a4(phal chen): pa'i phyogs su brten te/de bzhin gshegs pa la mngon du bltas nas seng+ge'i khri rin po che sna tshogs du ma'i padamo'i snying po can dag mngon par sprul te/skyil mo krung bcas te 'khod par gyur to//byang chub sems dpa' de dag 
65a5(phal chen): thams cad kyang spyod yul rgya mtsho cha chen po yongs su dag pa rnams so//sgo kun nas chos kyi snang ba rab tu thob pa rnams so//de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod yul chags pa med pa'i rjes su 'brang ba rnams so//tha dad pa yang 
65a6(phal chen): dag par shes pa thams cad chos rgya mtsho la zhugs pa rnams so//rnam par thar pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i snang ba rab tu thob pa rnam so//sgo kun nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyings rab tu thob pa rnams so//gzugs 
65a7(phal chen): kyi sgo rgya mtsho thams cad kyis chos rgya mtsho rab tu 'dzin pa rnams so//dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes kyi sa la rab tu gnas pa rnams so//dga' ba'i shugs rgya chen po rab tu thob pa rnams so/ /bsod nams dkyil 
65a8(phal chen): 'khor mu med pa yongs su dag pa'i rnams te/chos kyi dbyings dang nam mkha' la rnam par rgyu zhing sangs rgyas thams cad mchod pa mngon par bsgrub pa la rab tu brtson pa sha stag go/de nas byang chub sems dpa' rgya mtsho'i 
65b1(phal chen): blo gros rnam par rol pa'i ye shes mngon par shes pa'i rgyal pos sangs rgyas kyis mthus 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas rnam par sangs 
65b2(phal chen): rgyas mnyam nyid mkhyen//chags med rnyed pas nam mkha' lta bu ste//phyogs rnams zhing kun rab tu byung bas nas//'byam klas 'khor na bzhugs pa rnam par mdzes//bde gshegs rang byung mtha' dang dbus med de//thams 
65b3(phal chen): cad phyogs kyi chos dbyings kun rgyas par//dbang phyug chen po kun gyis mchod bskor cing //shing gi rgyal po kun gyi drung na bzhugs//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul stobs dag gis//skad cig sems la zad med yon 
65b4(phal chen): tan gzugs//rnam thar gzhan dang gzhan dag thob pas mthong //de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul ni mtha' yas so//bskal pa rgya mtshor sngon chad spyod pa'i tshe//de bzhin gshegs pas srid pa rgya mtshor 'dir//sna tshogs 
65b5(phal chen): thabs kyi sems can smin byas pa//de dag rgyal ba de yi chos 'dzin to//phongs dag dri med 'khor rnams 'khod pa dag/thams cad sangs rgyas lta zhing rnam par gnas//pad+mo'i snying po pad+mo gdan bzhugs pa//tshogs kyi 
65b6(phal chen): nang na rgyal ba mngon par mdzes//sangs rgyas gdan bzhugs pa'i seng+ge'i khri//rin chen nor bu'i rgyal po'i 'od 'phro zhing //spos 'phro sprin rnams mtha' yas rab tu 'byung //snying po du ma'i me tog phreng sbyangs pa'o//rin chen 
65b7(phal chen): 'od 'phro'i sprin ni rtag tu 'gyed//'od zer sgron ma rgya chen rab tu 'bar//rta babs rnam pa sna tshogs rnams kyis brgyan//thub pa de bzhugs mngon par rab tu mdzes//sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong ba rab tu 'gyed//rin chen 
65b8(phal chen): tshon du sna tshogs pad+mo 'byung //nor bu'i rgyal pos bskor rnams bkang nas brgyan//sangs rgyas de bzhugs rnam par rab tu mdzes//zla gam rin chen mang pos yongs su bzung //rdo rje 'od 'phro snang ba'i snying pos brgyan//
66a1(phal chen): @#//gtsug na nor bu'i byang chub sems dpas bzung //sangs rgyas de bzhugs rnam par shin tu mdzes//phyogs bcur sprul pa'i dra ba rab bkye zhing //de bzhin gshegs pa'i smon lam sgra yang sgrog/
66a2(phal chen): gzugs brnyan bzhin du zhing rnams kun na snang //thub pa de 'dra'i seng+ge'i gdan la bzhugs//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sgra dbyangs kun tu sgrogs pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du yod pa thams cad 
66a3(phal chen): la rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//'di ni bde gshegs sangs rgyas byin rlabs te//byang chub spyod pa sngon chad spyad pa dang //sangs rgyas mchod pa'i mchod pa du ma dag/rgyal ba'i gdan 
66a4(phal chen): las de dag thams cad snang //spos 'od snang ba nor bu'i rgyal po can//me tog snying po seng+ge'i gdan gyi 'khor//rgyan rnams thams cad gzugs brnyan gyur pa dang //'khor rnams de dag thams cad kun bkang ste//skad 
66a5(phal chen): cig skad cig rgyan gyi kha dog kun//rnam pa du mar sangs rgyas gdan las snang //sems can ji ltar mos pa de dag kyang //'khor cing sangs rgyas tshad med gdan la lta//rin chen lnga ldi pad+mo mang btags pa//phye zhing byang 
66a6(phal chen): chub sems dpa' mang po rnams//sgra dbyangs yid du 'ong ba rab 'byin cing //sangs rgyas gdan la bzhugs la stod pa byed//sangs rgyas bsod nams tshad med nam mkha' bzhin//'di dag rgyan kun de las rab tu 
66a7(phal chen): byung //sa rnams re re'i rgyan rnams gang yod pa//'gro ba kun gyis de dag bsam mi nus//mi phyed mtshams kyi rdo rje sna tshogs pa//'khor yangs rnam dag shin tu mnyam pa ste//nor bu'i dra ba sna tshogs kun tu bkram//
66a8(phal chen): byang chub shing gis yi 'khor kun to//sa phyogs gang na rgyal ba'i gdan yod pa//kha dog mtha' yas rgya chen sna tshogs te//me tog mngon gtor rin chen rnam par bkram//gser mtshon mdzes pas de ni rab tu brgyan//
66b1(phal chen): sa yis lha mo de dag rab dga' zhing //skad cig sems kyis mi zad sa yang ston//kun kyang rgyal ba'i zhal du mngon lta zhing //kha dog thams cad rgyan gyi sprin gtong ngo //rin chen sgron ma mang po gsal bar 'bar//spos mang dri 
66b2(phal chen): zhim mang po rtag tu 'byung //dus dang tshod kyi rnam pa mang ston cing //lha rnams kun kyang sangs rgyas mchod pa byed//phyogs rnams bcu na zhing rnams ji snyed pa//de dag sa rgyan phun sum tshogs yod pa//de dag kun kyang sa 
66b3(phal chen): dkyil 'di na snang //sangs rgyas byin rlabs mnyam pa med pas phyung //de nas byang chub sems dpa' nor bu rin po che sna tshogs kyi 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gtsug phud kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'od kyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams 
66b4(phal chen): cad la rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sngon cad spyad pa spyod pa'i tshe de dag/zhing kun phun sum tshogs pa mthong ba rnams//snying po byang chub 'khor gyi sa 'di la//mi zad sa dang de dag rab tu 
66b5(phal chen): snang //rnam par yangs pa gang na snang ba yi//sa la rin chen mdzes kun rnam par bkram//rin chen mang pos dkyil 'khor kun tu gtor//sangs rgyas 'od kyi sprin kyang rab tu snang //sa rnams kun brgyan shing gi dam 
66b6(phal chen): pa'i drung //kun nas nor bu'i rgyal pos rgyan byas las//bsod nams 'od kyi dbyangs kyi 'brug sgra 'byung //sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul tshul yang rab tu brjod//rin chen mang po sgron ma'i char phab cing //rin chen mdzes par 
66b7(phal chen): bkod pa rnam par mdzes//sgra dbyangs kun gyi 'brug sgra shin tu sgrog/sa yi lha rnams sa yang yang dag ston//rin chen 'od 'phro sgron ma rnam par snang //'od zer sprin ni sa las rab tu 'byung //nor bu dra ba sil ma kun nas 
66b8(phal chen): bkram//rin chen sna tshogs rigs kyi yan lag brgyan//sa rnams kun nas mdzes pa 'di dag ltos//rin chen mang po tshon bzang brgyan tu bkye//'gro ba dag la chos kyi rgya mtsho sgrog/de dag chos nyid rnam par dag pa 
67a1(phal chen): @#//mthong //ma lus phyogs na sangs rgyas thams cad kyi//byang chub shing gi phun sum tshogs pa dag/sa yi dkyil 'khor 'di na kun snang ste//de dag chos nyid rnam par dag pa brjod//sangs rgyas 
67a2(phal chen): gdan las chos rnams rgya mtsho yi//chos sgo bde bar gshegs pas gang gsungs pa//sems can kun gyi bsam pa ji bzhin du//sa 'khor rnams kyang kun nas rab tu 'byung //dri zhim 'od rnams rtag tu 'byung 
67a3(phal chen): bas na//de dag ji bzhin snod kyi 'gro ba 'gengs//srog chags rnams kyi nyon mongs zhi bar byed//sa 'khor de nas de 'dra rab tu skrag/sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul phun sum gang dag dang //dri med sa ni gang gis mdzes 
67a4(phal chen): gyur pa'i//phun sum tshogs pa'i rgyan rnams re re zhing //bskal pa bye ba dag tu'ang brjod mi nus//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes chen po'i nyi mas rab tu gnon pa'i blo gros kyis//sangs rgyas kyi mthus 
67a5(phal chen): 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa'i de dag smras so//sangs rgyas rmad ga cad ces bya ba shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////dum bu dang po//bam po lnga pa/
67a6(phal chen): 'jig rten kun gyi bsam pa tshim mdzad cing //sangs rgyas chos kyi gzhal med khang na mdzes//'byam klas phyogs rnams kun tu mngon sum du//sangs rgyas 'gro gtso gzhal med khang na snang //rin chen 
67a7(phal chen): kun gyis snying po rab tu brgyan//rgyan rnams kun gyis de bzhin mdzes shing 'bad//sangs rgyas pho brang 'od ni bsam mi khyab//de na bzhugs shing sangs rgyas rab tu mdzes//ka ba'i rgyan ni nor bu sna tshogs 
67a8(phal chen): rten dang phyogs rnams kun sgor rta babs sprin kyang bris//stegs bu'i phreng ba kun nas brgyan tu bkod//gser gyi dril bu'i sprin kyang rnam par rgyu//me tog bla re rnam par sna tshogs ltos//rin chen shing sdong 
67b1(phal chen): dar gyi bla re bres//nor bu thams cad sgo nas phreng ba 'bul//de nas ye shes rgya mtshor rab tu mdzes//spos dri rgyal mtshan rin chen dra ba ste//'od zer sgron ma'i sprin gyis kun tu g.yogs//rgyan rnams sna chog 
67b2(phal chen): kun nas shin tu bres//de na sangs rgyas rab tu 'phags pa bzhugs//phyogs rnams kun sgo sprul pa'i sprin gyi rnams//dbyangs kyis 'jig rten khyab par grag byed cing //'byam klas phyogs kyi sems can rnam 
67b3(phal chen): 'dul ba//sangs rgyas pho brang 'di na de dag grag/me tog shing dang rin chen spos brdal ba//'phags par phyogs rnams kun tu mngon par mdzes//de las dus gsum zhing rnams thams cad kyang //gzugs brnyan 
67b4(phal chen): gyur par de dag rab tu snang //phyogs rnams kun tu rin chen mang bkye ba//de dag sna tshogs mdzes par rnam par 'bar//phyogs rnams kun tu skar dgung mdzes par brgyan//rta babs mda' yab sna tshogs mang 
67b5(phal chen): pos brgyan//dri med 'od zer kun nas snang ba yi//sangs rgyas pho brang rnam pa bsam yas te//gzhal med khang mang thams cad de la snang //de dag kun na'ang rgyal ba bzhugs shing mdzes//phyogs rnams kun na 
67b6(phal chen): 'khor rnams ji snyed pa//rgyal ba'i pho brang 'dir ni kun zhugs kyang //mtha' med dbus med mu'ang de bzhin du//rang byung sangs rgyas pho brang de na bzhugs//de nas byang chub sems dpa' yon tan rin po che bsam gyis 
67b7(phal chen): mi khyab pa ye shes rgya mtsho dpal gyis sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'khor 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho yod pa de dag thams cad la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas zhing rdul 
67b8(phal chen): shin tu phra snyed kyi//bsod nams rgya mtsho rnam par rab sbyangs te//sangs rgyas rdzu 'phrul smon lam yang dag byung //snying po byang chub dri med rnam par mdzes//spos ni ma thams cad rtsa ba yang dag rgyu//yid bzhin nor 
68a1(phal chen): @#//mchog rgyan pos kun nas brgyan//rdo rje bye ma'i nor bu'i rgyal po bras//shing sdong rtsa ba rin chen kun las byas//rdo rje phye ma'i nor bu 'khor lo 'byung //lhun rnams thams cad rin chen rgyan 
68a2(phal chen): kyis brgyan//yal ga sdug po sprin ltar kun nas 'khyil//snying po byang chub de na rgyal ba mdzes//yal ga dog pa kun nas mngon pa ste//'khor rnams snum 'jam phyogs rnams kun tu 'phags//me tog 'bras bu rin 
68a3(phal chen): rnams kun kyang bgyid//snying po byang chub rgya chen bsam mi khyab//'od rnams bkye ba phyogs su rnam par mdzes//dri ma med pa'i 'od 'bar zad mi shes//yal ga'i sgo nas sprin kun char yang 'bebs//snying po byang chub 
68a4(phal chen): smon lam yangs pa byung //me tog nor dang rin chen mi zad nor//mu tig nor bu phyogs rnams kun nas brgyus//phye ma nor gyi gnas kyi sprin 'gyed de//snying po byang chub phyogs rnams kun tu 'byin//bde gshegs 
68a5(phal chen): snying po byang chub 'di la bltos//pad+mo'i dra ba nor bu dri med mang //rin chen 'od zer dkyil 'khor kun nas 'phro//dril bu'i dra ba sprin gyi sgra dbyangs 'byin//phyogs bcu'i shing bzang gzugs rnams gang yod pa//
68a6(phal chen): 'byam klas zhing gi ma lus thams cad dag/byang chub shing la de dag kun nas snang //de drung sangs rgyas dri med rnam par mdzes//yal ga kun nas rin chen mi zad nor//byang chub sems dpa' dag kyang rtag 
68a7(phal chen): 'byung zhing //bsod nams rgya chen dkyil 'khor char phab ste//phyogs bcur rgyal ba mchod phyir de dag 'dod//de bzhin gshegs pa sngan cad cir gyur dang //byang chub spyod las byung ba 'di dag ltos//yal

85a5(phal chen): bya ba'i phyir/smin mtshams kyi mdzod spu nas 'od zer chen po byang chub sems dpa' thams cad ye shes kyi snang bas phyogs rnam par ston pa'i snying po zhes bya ba rab tu bkye'o//'od zer rin po che 
85a6(phal chen): thams cad kha dog sgron ma'i sprin du brdal ba/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du rab du snang ba de byung nas/tshul thams cad kyi phyogs 'byam klas pa rab tu snang bar byas te/phyogs thams cad kyi 
85a7(phal chen): dbyings rgya mtsho rnams rab tu snang bar byas nas sems can rgya mtsho rnams rab tu snangs bar byas te/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho thams cad rab tu bstan nas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing mang po thams 
85a8(phal chen): cad rab tu bskyod pa'i rgyan gyis tshul thams cad kyi rab tu bskyod nas/sangs rgyas kyi sprin 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed/rdul shin tu phra ba thams cad dang mnyam 
85b1(phal chen): pa dag tu rab tu spros nas/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang mos pa ci lta ba rgya mtsho dag tu de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi chos kyi 'khor lo'i sprin gyi char dus gsum gyi rdul shin tu phra 
85b2(phal chen): ba dang mnyam pa phab ste/dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa rgya mtsho rab tu bstan nas/sems can thams can ma lus par 'khor ba thams cad las rab tu gdon par bya ba'i phyir/
85b3(phal chen): mos pa ji bzhin du byung ba dang mthun pa'i sprin gyi char rab tu dbab pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi smon lam ma lus pa'i sprin gyi char rab du dbab pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 
85b4(phal chen): du byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i 'khor dag tu dga' bar phrad par byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas te/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar padamo chen po chu bo rin 
85b5(phal chen): po che thams cad las bsgrubs pa/nang rim nor bu'i rgyal po'i mdzod sna tshogs las byas pa lo ma rin po che thams cad kyi rigs kyi char 'bebs pa/ze ba'i phreng ba'i rgyan spos rin po che thams cad 
85b6(phal chen): las byas pa/snying po'i 'dzam bu chu klung gser gyi tshon gyis rab tu mdzes par bris pa/'od kyi 'khor rnam par dag cing 'od zer gyi dra bas rnam par snang bar byas pa'i sprin dang ldan pa/rgyan thams 
85b7(phal chen): cad rab tu 'byung ba sems kyi skad cig la sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'khrul pa mtha' yas ston pa'i sgra rab tu 'byung gnor bu'i rgyal po mtha' yas pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi sku'i gzugs brnyan gyi 
85b8(phal chen): sprin gyis thams cad du gang bar byed pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dang /smon lam thams cad rab tu ston pa'i dbyangs 'byin par gyur te byang ngo//padamo mtha' yas chen po byung ba'i rjes su 
86a1(phal chen): @#//byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad la pham pa med pa'i dbyangs zhes bya ba/byang chub sems dpa' 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed dang lhan cig tu 
86a2(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das kyi mdzod spu de nyid nas byung ste/bcom ldan 'das la lan du mar g.yas phyogs su bskor ba byas nas/bcom ldan 'das la phyag 'tshal te/padamo chen po de'i snying po la 
86a3(phal chen): 'jug go/byang chub sems dpa' 'jug rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rtul shin tu phra ba snyed po de dag la 'khod do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos rnams chad la pham pa med pa'i dbyings 
86a4(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings rgya che zhing tshad med pas rab tu dga' ba'i shugs shin tu skyes pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi spyod yul gyi rjes su song ba'i ye shes can/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi 
86a5(phal chen): sku rgya mtsho bsam gyis mi khyab pa la rab tu zhugs pa'i mthu dang ldan pa/zhing 'byams klas pa thams cad kyi de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi zhabs kyi drung du phyin pa dang /ba spu thams cad las 
86a6(phal chen): sprul pa'i sprin zad mi shes pas kun tu khebs par byed pa/sems kyi skad cig thams cad la dmigs pa/thams cad chos kyi dbyings thams cad la dus gsum khyab par byed pa'i spyod yul 
86a7(phal chen): gyis rnam par spyod pa/phyogs thams cad kyi de bzhin gshags pa'i byin gyis brlabs 'chang ba/ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rab tu 'jog cing phyi ma'i mtha'i bskal pa'i mur thugs par 
86a8(phal chen): ting nge 'dzin dang /rnam par thar pa la rnam par spyod pa/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan rgya mtsho mtha' yas pa thams cad dang chos kyi dbyings kyi lus dang ldan pas de bzhin gshegs pa'i rdzu 'phrul 
86b1(phal chen): dang /rnam par 'phrul pa mtha' dang dbus med pa la rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//sangs rgyas kun ni chos kyi dbyings kyi gsal//'gro ba kun la rgyal ba mngon 
86b2(phal chen): sum ste//ji ltar mos pa'i sems can rgyas bkang nas//snying po byang chub de la rgyal ba bzhugs//zhing rnams kun gyi rdul phran mchog snyed kyi//rgyal ba spu rtse kun la rab bzhugs te//byang chub sems 
86b3(phal chen): dpa' 'khor gyis kun nas bskor//kun kyang bzang po'i spyod pa mchog ston to//spu gcig rtse la zhing mang rgya mtsho ste//byang chub snying po padama'i gdan la bzhugs//chos dbyings rgya chen thams cad kun 
86b4(phal chen): bkang ste//spu rnams kun la rnam par 'phrul pa ston//zhing rnams re re dag na rab bzhugs shing //zhing rnams 'byam klas kun tu rgyas mdzad pas//phyogs bcu'i byang chub sems dpa'i sprin 
86b5(phal chen): gyi rnams//kun kyang snying po byang chub drung du 'dod//zhing rnams kun gyi rdu phran mchog snyed kyi//byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan rgya mtsho'i 'od//sangs rgyas kyi ni 'khor du nye bar 'dod//chos 
86b6(phal chen): dbyings 'byam klas rtag tu rgyas par 'gengs//zhing rnams thams cad gzugs brnyan rab tu ston//chos dbyings rdul rnams ye shes spyod yul can//kun kyang bzang po'i sbyod la rab tu gnas//
86b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas kun gyi 'khor du yang dag byung //byang chub sems dpa' ye shes mchog yul can//sangs rgyas chos sprin nyan cing rab tu mkhas//sangs rgyas kun gyi 'khor rnams kun na gnas//
86b8(phal chen): zhing rnams kun tu bskal pa rgya mtshor spyod//rgya mtsho'i chos la rnam par snang bar spyod//de dag rgyal ba'i sa rnams rgya mtshor bzhugs//sangs rgyas chos rnams gzhan dang gzhan byung ba//

91a8(phal chen): bla ma brdal ba//de dag kun gyi 'khor rnams su//shin tu bstan pa'i chos 'byung ngo //de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dbyings na//'khor ba'i zhing rnams bsam mi khyab//
91b1(phal chen): de dag kun tu chos nyid 'di//'jig rten mgon po rnams kyang ston//tha dad med pa'i chos kyi dbyings//chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu 'khor//tshig gi tshul gyi rnam pa yis//chos bstan

97b7(phal chen): ston//skad cig sems la bskal ba mang po de bzhin ston//zhing rnams thams cad 'byam klas pa na rnam grags shing //rnam par 'phrul ba dam pa ston par rab tu snang //phyogs rnams kun gyi zhing gi dbyings

98a1(phal chen): @#//gzhan dang gzhan la rab tu zhugs//rang gi byang chub spyod pas sngan cad spyod bstod//de ltar zhing rnams kun tu bsam yas rnam 'phrul ba//phyogs rnams rgya che kun tu bkang ste rab tu

98a5(phal chen): rnams kun na snang //phyogs bcu kun tu yon tan grags pas khyab//ye shes rgya mtsho rab tu snang bar mnga'//rgyal ba thams cad sku thob phyir spyod pa//phyogs bcu'i yon tan rgya mtsho yod pa'i rnams//

100a7(phal chen): rab tu bstan pa dang /sems can tham sa cad kyi dbang po rgya mtsho la sangs rgyas kyi chos tshul kun tu snang ba yang dag par bskyed pa dang /sems can thabs cad kyi bsam pa rgya mtsho'i sgrib

101a1(phal chen): @#//sems can rgya mtsho 'byung ba bsam mi khyab//sangs rgyas dus dang 'jug pa dang //rtsa zab//srid pa'i gnas can dad 'gyur ba yis//sangs rgyas byang chub shes par mi nus so//

102b7(phal chen): skad cig sems kyi dus gsum byung ba yi//zhing 'byung tshul dang 'jug pa kun kyang ston//sangs rgyas rnams kyis dus gsum zhing ston pa//rnam par snang mdzad rgya mtsho'i zhing mi zad//
102b8(phal chen): tshul gyi khyad par mtha' yas sna tshogs pas//rdul rnams kun la mtha' yas rgyan gyi rnams//sangs rgyas skad cig gcig la byin gyis rlob//rgyan gyis rnam pa tshul kun rab tu 
103a1(phal chen): @#//mkhyen//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la gnas pa shin tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho de ni de 'jig rten gyi khams/rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu brab sked de 'di ltas 
103a2(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho la la ni rgyan thams cad kyi rgya mtsho kun la rab tu gnas so//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho la la ni nam mkha'i dbyings kyi gnas la rab tu gnas so//'jug 
103a3(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rin po che thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas so//'jog rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'od dkyil 'khor rgya mtsho la rab 
103a4(phal chen): tu gnas so//'jig rten kyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni ring po che thams cad kyi kha dog la rab tu gnas so//'dzag rten gyi khams rgya mtsho ba la ni sangs rgyas thams chad kyi 'od dkyil 'khor rgyal mtsho la rab 
103a5(phal chen): la rab tu gnas so//'jing rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la yin sgyu ma lta bu'i las kyi byung ba lha ma yin dang mthu'o che lta bu'i lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas/'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 
103a6(phal chen): la la ni/'jig rten gyi khams dbang po thams cad kyi lus kyi gzugs brnyan rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas sa/'jig rten gyis khams rgya mtsho la yin byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 
103a7(phal chen): kyi lus rgya mtsho la rab tu gnas /'jig rten gyi khams rgya gam tsho la la ni byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i smon lam gyi byung ba'i rgyan nam pa thams cad dang ldan pa rgya mtsho la 
103a8(phal chen): rab tu gnas te/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rab tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya 'tsho re re zhing //yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu brab snying 
103b1(phal chen): do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho rab tu gnas pa shin tu bstan pa'i don 'di nyid yongs su bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi mthus 
103b2(phal chen): de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//kun kyang zhing rnams rgya mtsho 'di dag rin chen rang bzhin dri med pa//rnam dag sna tshogs nor bu kun 'bar 'di zer rgya mtsho'i 'od ldan ltos//
103b3(phal chen): 'di dag rgyal ba'i byin rlabs rgyu yis dbyings legs 'khor bar snang //de dag gis ni phyogs bcu'i nam mkha'i dbyings rnams gzhi kun rab tu khyab//zhing rnams rgya mtsho la la dri med 'od can 
103b4(phal chen): nam mkha'i dbyings la gnas//snang ba'i snying po 'od can nor bu'i rgya mtsho dag la legs par gnas//'gro ba de dbus rgyal ba 'khor la chos kyi 'khor lo bzang po bskor//'di ni sangs rgyas 
103b5(phal chen): yul yin sems can rgya chen shin tu mgu ba byed pa'o//la la me tog sgron ma kun tu rgyas pa'i mu tig nor bus brgyan//'od zer dra ba'i kun nas sprin 'bab spros 'phro sprin gyi 'od can te//
103b6(phal chen): zhing mang mtha' dang dbus med rgya chen pad mo'i rgya mtsho dag la gnas//sangs rgyas rnams kyi rab brgyan zhing rnams 'byam klas kun kyang rab tu mdzes//zhing rnams rgya mtsho dkyil 
103b7(phal chen): 'khor dbyings rnams la la sangs rgyas byin la gnas//yangs shing rin chen mang ldan rgya chen de dag byang chub sems dpa' gang //lag na rdo rje rgya mtsho mang tshogs lha dbang lus la 
103b8(phal chen): de bzhin gnas//de nas bcom ldan 'das rnam par snang mdzad kyi 'khor lo rtag tu 'khor//nor bu'i shing dang spos kyi 'od 'phro'i sprin rnams mnyam pa'i dbyings la gnas//chu yi 
104a1(phal chen): @#//phung po dag dang rdo rje'i rgya mtsho brtan la'ang de bzhin gnas/la la gos dang brag dang rgyal mtshan me tog rgya chen dag la gnas//kun na'ang rnam 'phrul 'di dag rgya chen rnam 
104a2(phal chen): par snang mdzad ston pa mdzad//zhing rnams ring dang thung dang gling gi gzugs dang rnam pa sna tshogs ste//sna tshogs rgya chen snying po dri ma bral ba yongs dag dri med snang //rnam pa sna tshogs de dag smon 
104a3(phal chen): lam rgya mtsho'i dbyings la rab tu gnas//sangs rgyas sprin gyis kun nas log par rtag tu nam mkha'i dbyings la gnas//zhing rnams rgya mtsho 'byam klas la la spyi'u tshugs yod dang med la 
104a4(phal chen): gnas//byang chub sems dpa'i cod pan rin chen 'khor los bzung bas dag par gnas//de na sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul rgya chen thams cad kun nas rab tu snang //rin chen sangs rgyas gsung 
104a5(phal chen): rnams kun tu gtong ldan las kyis de dag sprul//chos kyi dbyings mnyam zhing rnams 'byam klas mig yor sgyu ma dang yang 'dra//mig 'phrul rnam phye rnam dag dri med sems kyis shin tu bskyed 
104a6(phal chen): pa ste//nam mkha'i dbyings rnams sna tshogs rgyan gyi snying po gnas 'gyur 'di la snang //las kyi yul ni bsam gyis mi khyab 'di dag bde bar gshegs pas bstan//skad cig sems kyis 
104a7(phal chen): zhing rnams 'byam klas 'gro ba'i kun dang mnyam pa'i rnams//zhing rdul kun la kun tu bzang po rtog par rab tu bsgrub byed cing //de na bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro ba smin par bya phyir spyad pa spyod//
104a8(phal chen): chos dbyings rgyas par bag bste de dag kun tu rnam 'phrul rgya chen ston//rdul rnams kun na chos dbyings yangs pa'i zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun kyang gnas//sangs rgyas sprin gyis byin gyis 
104b1(phal chen): byin gyis rlabs pas kun kyang yog cing yongs su rgyas//ji ltar rdul phran gcig la rnam 'phrul de bzhin 'gro ba kun na'o//rnam par snang ba'i sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul rgya chen rab tu ston pa mdzad//zhing 
104b2(phal chen): rnams 'byam klas thams cad mig yor lta bu sgyu ma smig rgyu 'dra//snang ba med cing 'byung med phyogs bcu dag na 'gro dang 'ong ba med//mkha' la gnas pa de dag rtag par 'byung zhing 'jig par de 
104b3(phal chen): bzhin 'gyur//shin tu yangs pa de dag smon lam rnam sbyangs las kyis yongs su bzung //kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho rnams kyi dbyings ni rnam pa sna tshogs ste//'jig 
104b4(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa la la ni kun nas zlum pa'o//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho' byam klas pa la la ni kun gnas gru bzhi'o//'jig rten gyi khams rgyu mtsho 'byam klas pa la la 
104b5(phal chen): ni nam mkha'i rnam pa du mar rnam par phye ba'o//'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgya mtsho 'byam klas pa las la ni kun nas 'khyil ba'i dbyibs su 'dug go/'jig rten gyi khams rgyal mtsho 'byam klas pa la las ni 
104b6(phal chen): 'od 'phro ba'i dbyibs su 'dug go//'jig rten gyi khams mtsho 'byam klas pa la lan shing sna tshogs kyi lhun gyi dbyibs su 'dug go/'jig rten gyi khams rgya cho 'byam klas pa la la la ni me tog 
104b7(phal chen): sna tshogs kyi dbyibs su 'dug go/'ji rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'a byam klas pa la la ni khyin sna tshogs kyi dbyibs su 'dog go/'jig rten gyi khamasargya mtsho 'byam klas pa la ni sems 
104b8(phal chen): can sna tshogs kyi bus kyi dbyibs su 'dug go/'ji rten gyi khams rgya mtsho' 'byam klas pa la la ni mtshan sna tshogs kyi dbyibs su'adug ste/de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams kyi dbyibs 
105a1(phal chen): @#//shin tu bstan pa ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba sked do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho 'di nyid yongs 
105a2(phal chen): su bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//rnam pa sna tshogs rgya mtsho'i zhing la ltos//rnam 
105a3(phal chen): phye sna tshogs mtha' yas dbyibs su gnas//dbyibs kyis rnam par phye ba sna tshogs te//phyogs rnams kun nas zhing rnams rgya mtsho snang //la la zlum po pa la zur gsum ste//de dag 
105a4(phal chen): la la phyogs dang gu bzhir gnas//nor bu padamo 'khor lo'i yan lag brgyad//kun kyang las rnams rgya mtsho rnam par bris//rdul med 'od kyi dbyibs dang tshul bzhin ci//gser las 
105a5(phal chen): grub pa rnam phye sna tshogs te//sgo mang mtha' yas pa ni rgyu zhing 'jug/las rnams rgya mtsho bsam pa dag pas gnas//zhing rnam rgya mtsho sna tshogs snying po can//nam mkha' 'di la 
105a6(phal chen): ji ltar sprin gnas bzhin//de dag rin chen mang zhing rnam par phye//de dag kun ni sangs rgyas rnam par mdzes//zhing rnams 'di kun sems kyi rnam par brtags//sna tshogs rgyan 
105a7(phal chen): dang snang bas kun nas gnas//rnam par 'phrul ba gzhan dang gzhan dag gis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho sangs rgyas ston pa mdzad//bde dang sdug bsngal myong bar gyur pa ni//rnam par dag 
105a8(phal chen): dang nyon mongs tshul bzhin te//chos nyid 'di ni rtag tu yod pa yin//de ltar las rnams rgya mtsho bsam mi khyab//skra gcig rtse la bsam gyis mi khyab cing //thams cad rdul med sna tshogs 
105b1(phal chen): dbyibs su gnas//kun nas sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad bzhugs//'khor rnams rgya mtshor chos rnams rab tu ston//rdul gcig rtse la phra dang yangs pa'i rnams//thams cad rdul snyed 
105b2(phal chen): kha dog kun dang ldan//kha dog dbyibs rnams kun kyang rnam par brtags//de dag kun na rgyal pa'i 'khor lo bskor//rdul rnams kun la rab tu zhing bstan pa//de dag smon lam rnam par sprul 
105b3(phal chen): pa yin//nam mkha'i dbyibs su las kyis sprul pa ni//sna tshogs sems dang bsam pas rnam par brtags//sems can kun mnyam rdul rnams thams cad la//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul 
105b4(phal chen): pa sprul pa dag/rnam par snang mdzad chos nyid de yin te//rdul 'phran kun la zhing rnams shin tu gnas//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i lhun dag ni gzhan dang gzhan du shes 
105b5(phal chen): par byas ste//de ltar 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho la la ni rin po che'i rgyun thams cad kyi lhun can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho la la rin po che gcig las rnam pa sna tshogs su bkod pa'i rgyan 
105b6(phal chen): gyis lhun can no//'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rin po chos thams cad kyi 'od zer gyi lhun can no//'jig rtenakyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni'od kha dog sna tshogs kyi lhun can no//'jig 
105b7(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rgyan thams cad kyi mdog gi lhun can no//'jig rtenagi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rdo rje'i gzhi mi shigs pa'i lhun can no//'jig rten:gyi khams rgya mtsho la la nisangs 
105b8(phal chen): rgyas kyi rdzu 'phrul dang byin gyis rlabs kyi lhun can no//'jigrtenagyi khams rgya mtsho la la'i rin po che'i mtshan nyid kyi lhun can no//'jigartenagyi khams rgya mtsho la lani sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa'i 
106a1(phal chen): @#//lhun can no//'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni nyin no du'i dkyil so so gyi lhun can no//'jig rten kyi khams rgya mtsho la ni shin tu phra ba 'dzin pa'i lhun 
106a2(phal chen): can no//'jig rten gyi kha mas rgya mtsho la la ni snang ba'i lhun can no//'jig rtenagyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni rin chen thams cad kyi kha dog 'od 'phro ba'i rnam pa'i lhun can no//'jig rtan 
106a3(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni dri dang kha dog sna tshogs kyi spos gyi lhun can no//'jig rton gyi khams rgya mtsho la la ni ran po che thams cad kyi me tog cod pan gyi lhun can no//'jig 
106a4(phal chen): rten gyi khams rgya mtsho la la na rin po che thams cad kyi 'od kyi lhun can no//'jig rten
106a5(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho la ni rin po che thams cad gyi 'od kyi lhun can no//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i lhun shin tu bstan pa rgya mtsho 'di nyid 
106a6(phal chen): la rab tu gzung bar bya ba'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyi phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing gi rgya mtsho rnams la ni//
106b1(phal chen): rin chen rang bzhin kun nas gnas//rdo rje bzhin du mi shigs sra//rin chen pad mo rnams la gnas//la la 'od zer rang bzhin te//dag cing gzung med 'od zer 'byung //rgyan rnams kun gyi 'od zer 
106b2(phal chen): gyis//sna tshogs mkha' bzhin dri ma med//la la 'od kyi rang bzhin te//dag cir 'od gsal gsal la gnas//'od kyi sprin can sna tshogs 'od//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi gnas//zhing rnams 
106b3(phal chen): rgya mtsho la la ni//mi g.yor lta bur gnas pa ste//brjod du med cing gzung du med//smon lam dbang gi rab tu byung //la la nor bu mu tig rgyu//snying po thams cad rnam par 'bar//mu tig 'khor lo zhig 
106b4(phal chen): dag la//byang chub sems dpa' yongs su gang //rin chen 'od 'phro byung ba'i zhing //'od 'phro'i sprin gyis mngon 'bebs shing //rin chen thams cad sna tshogs ldan//thams cad las kyi sprul pa 
106b5(phal chen): yin//la la mtshan las byung ba ste//gzhi kun mtshan gyis kun tu bris//co dpan rnam pa sna tshogs bkod//sangs rgyas kun gyis mngon par sprul//rgya mtsho sems las byung ba yi//zhing rnams mos pa 
106b6(phal chen): nyid la gnas//yul na mi gnas rgya ma bzhin//yongs su rtogs pas yang dag rnyed//sangs rgyas sku'i 'od zer gyis//nor bu sna tshogs kun tu gnas//sangs rgyas sprin rnams rnam par snang //
106b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas kun gyis rab sprul nas//la la kun tu bzang po yis//zhing rnams rgya mtsho shin tu sprul//nam mkha'i rgyan rnams thams cad dang //smon lam rnams kyi rnam par bris//kye rgyal ba'i 
106b8(phal chen): sras dang de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa ni//'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu dra ba snyed du shes par bya ste/'di ltar dmigs pa'i rnam pa sna tshogs thams 
107a1(phal chen): @#//cad kyi sprin rab tu 'phros pa'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi yon tan gyi 'khor lo rjes su brjod cing rab tu byung ba'i rgyan dang /'di lta ste/'jig 
107a2(phal chen): rten gyi khams thams cad du rtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyi rnam par smin pa rgya mtsho las rab tu byung ba'i rgyan dang/'di lta ste byang chub sem dpa' thams cad kyi smon lam rgya mtsho'i klong 
107a3(phal chen): yongs su ston pa'i rgyan dang /'di lta te dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gzugs brnyan sna tshogs rnam par dmigs pa'i rgyan dang /'di lha ste sems kyi skad gcig la bskal pa mtha' 
107a4(phal chen): yas par rnam par 'phrul ba'i yul yongs su ston pa'i rgyan dang/'di lta ste/rgyan gyi dmigs pa thams cad las sangs rgyas kyi sku rab tu byung ba'i rgyan dang/'di lta ste rin po che dang spos thams cad kyi 
107a5(phal chen): sprin rab tu 'byung ba'i rgyan dang /'di lha sta snying po byang chub kyi rgyan dang /'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyi rnam par ston pa'i rgyan dang /'dilta ste byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang /spyod 
107a6(phal chen): pa yongs su ston pa'i rgyan dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de dag las sogs pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa ni/'jig rten gyi khams rgya ma tsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba slad 
107a7(phal chen): do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kunatu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mcho'i rgyan shin tu bstan pa'i de nyid yongs su bstan pa'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs 
107a8(phal chen): bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhing rnams rgya mtsho rgya chen mtha' yas te//rnam dag las kyis rnam pa sna tshogs gnas//phyogs bzhi zhing rnams 
107b1(phal chen): rgya mtsho rnams kun kyang //sna tshogs rgyan can yangs par kun nas gnas//kha dog sna tshogs yangs pa'i zhing gi rnams//rgya mtsho rtag par phyogs rnams kun du 'byung //rgyan dang rin chen 'od 'phro 
107b2(phal chen): sna tshogs sprin//sgra 'byin zhing gi dbyibs rnams khebs par byed//byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan rgya mtsho rnams//de dag kun kyang rgya mtsho'i zhing na grags//sna tshogs brgyan dang smon lam 
107b3(phal chen): sprin gyi rnams//de dag zhing mang rgya mtshor sgra yang 'byin//'gro ba'i lam rnams rgya mtsho yangs pa dag/rgyan gyi sprin rnams kun nas sgrar 'byin te//sems can de dag rnam smin ci 'dab//sangs 
107b4(phal chen): rgyas byin gyis kun nas sgra yang sgrog/dus gsum byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa yis//rnam 'phrul zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun na snang //dmigs pa re re la yang sangs rgyas kun//de 'dra'i zhing mang rgya 
107b5(phal chen): mtsho'i rgyan la ltos//'das pa'i bskal pa de bzhin ma 'ongs dang //phyogs bcu'i da ltar 'byung ba'i ji snyed pa'i//bskal pa de dag rgyan mang yangs pa yang //zhing rnams rgya mtsho re re dag na snang //dmig 
107b6(phal chen): pa kun la sangs rgyas sprin gyi rnams//'gro dang mnyam pas 'jig rten kun tu 'gengs//rnam par 'phrul zhing sems can rnam par 'dul//'di ni zhing rnams rgya mtsho'i rnam thar to//spos 'od sprin dang de 
107b7(phal chen): bzhin me tog klung //rgyan rnams sna tshogs thams cad kun nas kyang //rtag par kun tu khyab par rab tu byung //'di ni zhing rnams rgyal tsho'i rnam thar te//phyogs bcu kun gyi snying po byang chub rnams//
107b8(phal chen): sna tshogs rgyan can kun kyang ma lus par//thams cad rgya mtsho'i zhing 'dir byibs dag na//nam mkha'i sprin bzhin rnam par ston pa snang //rgyal sras kun tu bzang po thams cad kyi//zhing rnams 
108a1(phal chen): @#//mtha' las 'byam klas rgyan gyi rnams//'gro ba'i grangs snyed bskal pa rgya mtsho rnams sbyangs pa//zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun na rab tu snang //kye rgyas pa'i sras dag 'jig rten 
108a2(phal chen): gyi khams rgya mtsho yongs su dag par bya ba'i tshul rgya mtsho ni 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed du shes par bya ste/'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dge ba'i bshes 
108a3(phal chen): gnyen thams cad la brtan pa'i dge ba'i rtsa bas bskal pa mnyam pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyi chos dbyings kyi bsod nams rgya chen po'i sprin rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /byang chub 
108a4(phal chen): sems dpa' rnams kyi mos pa'i dkyil 'khor rgya chen po rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub semasadapa' rnams kyi byang chub sems dpa' thams cad rab tu brtan pa'i yul la rnam par spyod pa dang /
108a5(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' rnamskyi pha rol tu phyin pa rgya mtsho thams cad yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /yongs su sbyangs pa dang /byang chub semsdpa' rnams kyi byang chub sims dpa'i sa thams 
108a6(phal chen): cad kyi dbyings la rab tu 'god cing gzungs pa'i rnam par spyod pa dang /byang chub sems/dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa dang /'byung ba rgya mtsho la rnam par spyad pa dang /byang chub sem dpa' rnams 
108a7(phal chen): kyi smon lam rgya mtsho thams cad mngon par bsgrubs pa rnam par dag pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rgyan rgya mtsho thams cad mngonapar bsgrub pa rnam par dag pa la rab tu 'jug pa 
108a8(phal chen): dang /'di lta ste byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyi stobs kyi tshul yang dag par bsgrubs pa rnam par dag pa dang /de dag la sogs pa 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho yongs su dag par bya ba'i tshul rgya mtsho 
108b1(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho 'dul shin tu phra ba sked du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtshoyongs su dag par bya ba'i tshul rgya mtsho 'di nyid rnam par brtan 
108b2(phal chen): pa'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyi phyogs btsur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//zhi rnams rgya mtsho thams cad sna tshogs rgyan//smon lam tshul rnams rgya mtsho 
108b3(phal chen): kun gyis byung //kha dog mtha' yas las kyi rgya mtsho dag/zhing rnams rgya mtsho rtag tu 'od mang 'gyed//sngon cad dge ba'i bshes gnyen bsnyen bkur cing //bskal pa mnyam pa'i las dge yongs 
108b4(phal chen): sbyangs te//snying rje byams pas dgongs yangs kun bkang zhing //zhing rnams rgya mtsho yangs pa 'di kun sbyangs//chos sgo kun tu ting 'dzin sa kun gyi//sangs rgyas yon tan rgya mtsho yongs sbyangs 
108b5(phal chen): te//bsam gtan tshul dang rnam thar sa sbyang zhing //zhing rnams rgya cho sna tshogs yangs pa byung //mos pa'i dkyil 'khor mtha' yas stobs bskyed cing //rgyal ba'i mnyam nyid ma nor rtogs byas nas//
108b6(phal chen): bzod pa rgya mtsho'i tshul rnams yongs sbyangs zhing //rnam dag kha dog mtha' yas zhing rnams byung //kha sod nams sprin ni nam mkha' mnyam bskyed cing //'gro ba kun phan bde don spyod spyad de//bsod

109a1(phal chen): @#//sems can sbyangs pas na//'di 'dra yid 'ong rgya mtsho'i zhing rnams 'byung //rgyan rnams kun gyi tshul dang sa sbyangs shing //rgyal ba'i yon tan rgya mtsho kun zhugs nas//

109b1(phal chen): mtsho re rer sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshul rgya mtsho 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i rdul shin tu phra bsnyed du shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i tshul/rgya mtsho'i

110a1(phal chen): @#//yid bzhin rab tu sgrog/bsam yas bskal ba de dag gsung sgrog cing //sems can rgya mtshor thang pas na IIIIIIIIII khyab ste//'khor rnams

110a3(phal chen): mtsho kun tu rgyas bkang zhing //'gro ba kun la sangs rgyas mngon sum ste//sna tshogs rgyan mang du ma'i tshul dang ldan//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de la 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho'i bskal ba'i byin gyi rlabs

110b2(phal chen): pa 'di dag smras so//'jig rten rgya mtsho'i bskal pa tha dad de//sna tshogs tshul 'jug rgyan rnams gzhan dang gzhan//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams rgya mtsho kun bskang //bskal pa'i tshul ni rgya mtsho grangs kyang

110b5(phal chen): la la dri med nyon mongs can//la pa rnam dag nyon mongs rnam par dag/bskal pa med ni smon lam rgya mtsho'i tshul gyis byung //sems can 'du shes rnam pas tha dad tshul la 'jug/bskal pa zhing gi rdul

110b8(phal chen): pa kun las byung //dri med bskal pa la lar sangs rgyas gcig skye'o//bskal pa la lar mi'i gtso bo mtha' yas 'byung //skal ba tshul rnams thams cad sa tshul zang mi shes//smon lam stobs mthun 
111a1(phal chen): @#//bskal pa sna tshogs 'jug par 'gyur//bskal ba la mtha' las rnams kyang gcig tu bzhugs//bskal pa la gcig kyang bskal pa'i bye ba'i mthu//bskal ba rgya mtsho kun kyang sna

111b1(phal chen): can rgya mtsho rgya chen po byang chub kyi sems la gnas pas 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho shin tu dag par gnas pa dang /'di lta ste byang chub sems dpa' rnams 'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dang gzhan du

114a8(phal chen): ston to//de'i steng na rlung gi dkyil 'khor kun nas 'dzin pa'i shugs shes bya ba yod de/sprin gyi nor bu thams cad kyi ring ba kyi sprin gyi gzhi la ston to//de'i steng na rlug gi dkyil 'khor 
114b1(phal chen): kyi ma kun tu rgyu ba zhes bya ba yod de/rin po che thams cad kyi kha dog dang spos kyi khang pa brtsegs pa'i sprin gyi gzhis ston to//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag rlung gi dkyil 'khor ri rab kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed de dag 
114b2(phal chen): thams cad kyi steng ma ni rlung gi dkyil 'khor gzi brjid bzang po dpal gyi snying po zhes bya ste/nor bu kun nas snang ba'i rgyan dang ldan pa'i spos kyi chu rgya mtsho'i gzhis bston to//spos kye rgya rgya mtsho nor bu 
114b3(phal chen): zhun nas snang ba'i gzhis brgyan pa'i snying po zhes bya ba de las pad+mo chen po spos kyi rgyal mtshan khyer ba'i 'od zer dang ze ba sna tshogs shes bya ba mngon par byung ste/pad+mo chen po de la 'jig rten gyi khams 
114b4(phal chen): rgya mtsho gzhi dang snying po me tog gi rgyan gyis brgyan pa'i kun nas grub zhing 'dug pa rnam par dag pa shin tu brtan pa rdo rje dang nor bu thams cad kyi 'khor yug gis bskor ba/rim gyis gzhol bar 
114b5(phal chen): gnas shing rgyud kyi rgya mtsho sa la rab tu gnas pa 'di 'dug go/de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos yang 'jig rten gyi khams rgya mtsho gzhi dang snying po med tog gis rgyan kyis brgyan pa 'di 
114b6(phal chen): nyid kyi yon tan rgya mtsho yongs su dag pa'i phyogs 'di nyid brjod pa'i phyir sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur nam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//gzhi 
114b7(phal chen): rgyan me tog 'jig rten khams gang rgya mtsho de ni rin chen kun gyi snying po kha dog sna tshogs te//rdul rnams kun dang mnyam pa'i sngon chad sangs rgyas kyi//drung du sngon tshe rgya mtshor spyad pas

115a1(phal chen): @#//mtsho'i khams de yangs shing rnam par dag/sangs rgyas rnams ni snang ba la smon lam che mdzad de//'od snang btang ba yang 'di gyis na gnas pa ni//rlung gi phung pos btab kyang mi bskyod 
115a2(phal chen): mi g.yo ste//nor bu kun rgyan sangs rgyas gzugs brnyan snying po'o//me tog mdzod pa kye gtong ba rin chen yid 'ong bzhin//sngon gyi smon lam dbang gis mkha'i dbyings la gnas//rgya mtsho'i rgyan 
115a3(phal chen): rnams thams cad sna tshogs bstan par gnas//phyogs dang phyogs mtshams kun tu 'od kyi sprin kyang gtong //nor bu thams cad sgo nas byang chub sems dpa'i rnams//byung zhing 'od zer sprin 
115a4(phal chen): 'bar phyogs rnams kun tu 'dor//'od zer me tog tshon dang 'od zer 'khor lo'i tshogs//phyogs rnams ma lus kun gyi chos dbyings khyab par byed//rin chen kun gyi 'od zer sprin rnams bkye pa

115a7(phal chen): rnams kun kyang gnas//sprul pas 'od bkye chos kyi dbyings kyi tshad mnyam pa//'od zer sgo nas sangs rgyas thams cad mtshan kyang sgrag/'gro ba ma lus kun gyi bsam pa tshim byas nas//

115b1(phal chen): sprin rnams 'bar bar snang //rnam par snang mdzad chos dbyings rnam 'phrul zhing la ltos//bskal pa rgya mtshor 'gro ba'i sdug bsngal bsal byas te//smon lam yangs pa'i sprin ni chos kyi dbyings

115b5(phal chen): gyis gnon pa nor bu spos zhim po gzi brjid kyi 'khor lo rnam par snang ba'i snying po can//nor bu'i rgyal po rdo rje sra zhing mi shig pa ltar shin tu 'khrugs shing sra ba rin po che'i chu thams cad kun nas 'byung 
115b6(phal chen): ba rin po che'i zhing gi nags tshal gyi rgyan thams cad me tog shin tu rgyas pa/spos kyi rtsi mchog bzang po thams cad kyi rgyan gyis brgyan pa/spos kyi me tog dpal can shin tu bzang bas rnam par

116b1(phal chen): brnyan chul du nang na yod pa snang //thams cad gzugs su sku rnams mtha' yas te//sangs rgyas sprul mang kha dog mu med pas//thams cad phyogs dang chos dbyings kun bkang zhing //sems can

121a1(phal chen): @#//rin chen rnams las rtag par snang zhing grag/sangs rgyas rnam par snang mdzad dri med 'od//rgyan rnams yid nang na rnam par snang //'khor rnams

121b7(phal chen): rab tu gang ba'o//nor bu'i rgyal po'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi smon lam bskal ba thams cad du byin gyis rlobs pa'i sgra sgrogs pa'i sprin gyis yongs su gang ba'o//spos 'od 'phro 
121b8(phal chen): ba'i sprin gyis 'od gzer gyis rnam par snang bar byed pa'i sprin gyis mngon par khyab pa'o//spos kyi rgyal po yid du 'ong ba sna tshogs kyis rnam par rgyas pa'o//nor bu'i rgyal po 
122a1(phal chen): @#//rgyan bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i kha dog yang dag par ston pas mkhas pa nyid mngon par gang ba'o//gos sna tshogs sprin lta bus mngon par yog pa'o//nor

281b5(phal chen): med pa yi lus su bshad//'byung ba med cing bstan tu med//'jig tshogs mang po ltar med pa//sangs rgyas sku ni bla na med//sangs rgyas chos kyi rang bzhin nyid//de skad bden pa'i blos smras 
281b6(phal chen): te//de thos pas ni mig kyang sbyangs//shin tu 'od kyang rang zhing snang //de nas byang chub sems dpa' dam pa'i blo gros kyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas 
281b7(phal chen): te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad de smras pa/drin gzo med pa'i sems can phyir//skyes bu dang slong chen po'i mchog gi rnams//dpa' bo chen po sems can gtso bo dag/'od mdzad pa ni rab tu byung //
281b8(phal chen): thugs rje chen po skyed mdzad de//sems can khams la rnam gzigs kyang //srid pa'i bcings pas sdug bsngal ba'i//sems can rnams ni de nas gzigs//'jig rten lha dang bcas pa na//
282a1(phal chen): @#//yang dag sangs rgyas dpal mnga' ba//sems can gtso bo ma gtogs par//skyob pa gzhan ni gang yang med//gal te sangs rgyas ma skyes 
282a2(phal chen): pa//byang chub sems dpa' 'di 'dra dag/med na 'jig rten khams kun na//sems can bde ba gcig mi 'byung //sems can gtso bo 'dren mdzad pa//de bzhin gshegs pas skyes 
282a3(phal chen): pa dang //byang chub sems dpa' de 'dra dag/'byung ba 'jig rten bde ba'i rgyu//gang gis de bzhin gshegs mthong ba//de dag 'thob pa legs par 'thob//gang gis thos shing dad 'gyur 
282a4(phal chen): ba//de yang 'jig rten mchod rten no//gang gis sangs rgyas mthong gyur pa//bdag cag rnams kyis 'thob legs thob//'di 'dra ba yi tshul thos pas//kun kyang yang dag sangs 
282a5(phal chen): rgyas 'gyur//'das pa'i dus na'ang dpal 'di yod//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyis//shes rab mig ni rnam sbyangs pas//spyod yul 'di dag mthong bar 'gyur//rnam 
282a6(phal chen): par snang mdzad thub mthong nas//da rung phyir zhing dad pa skye//de yi ye shes mtha' yas pas//da dung brjod du yod pa min//gang zhig mchog gi blos smras pa//rgyal sras gzhan 
282a7(phal chen): gyis bshad pa rnams//de dag da rung bdag gis kyang //bskal pa bsam yas bshad par bya//ri rab kyi rtse mor de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang /byang 
282a8(phal chen): chub sems dpa'i tshogs kyi tshigs su bcad pa'i le'u ste/bcu dgu pa'o////bkra shis par gyur cig/
